TNO_is_iberian_nation_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = IBR
				original_tag = SPR
				original_tag = SPS
				original_tag = CTL
				original_tag = CNT
				original_tag = BSQ
				original_tag = FSR
				original_tag = GNS
				original_tag = TBA
				original_tag = POR
				original_tag = PPR
				original_tag = RPP
				original_tag = GAL
				original_tag = FZL
				original_tag = ADL
			}
		}

		set_country_flag = is_iberian_nation
	}
}

IBR_stability_update = {
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_ideas = {
			IBR_State_Exemplary #Exemplary
			IBR_State_Strong #Strong
			IBR_State_MedStrong #Stable
			IBR_State_Med
			IBR_State_MedWeak
			IBR_State_Weak
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBRIberiaStability < 21 } }
			add_ideas = IBR_State_Weak
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBRIberiaStability < 36 } }
			add_ideas = IBR_State_MedWeak
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBRIberiaStability < 51 } }
			add_ideas = IBR_State_Med
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBRIberiaStability < 71 } }
			add_ideas = IBR_State_MedStrong
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBRIberiaStability < 81 } }
			add_ideas = IBR_State_Strong
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = IBR_State_Exemplary
		}
		#KD's Notes - Someone wiser than me may get it to work in the future
		#divide_temp_variable = { IBR_stability_change = 100 }
		#add_stability = IBR_stability_change
	}
}

IBR_stability_change = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_stability_temp < 0 }
			is_ai = yes
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_stability_temp = 0.8 }
	}
	add_to_variable = { IBRIberiaStability = IBR_stability_temp }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = IBRIberiaStability
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_stability_temp > 10 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_add_stability_really_big_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_stability_temp > 5 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_add_stability_big_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_stability_temp > 1 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_add_stability_med_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_stability_temp > 0 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_add_stability_small_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_stability_temp < -10 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_sub_stability_really_big_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_stability_temp < -5 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_sub_stability_big_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_stability_temp < -1 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_sub_stability_med_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_sub_stability_small_tt
	}
	IBR_stability_update = yes
}

# Subsidies Effects

IBR_agri_misc_costs_change = {
	add_to_variable = { IBR_agri_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_agri_misc_costs_temp }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_agri_misc_costs_temp < 0 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_agri_misc_costs_temp = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_agri_misc_costs_decrease_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_agri_misc_costs_increase_tt
	}
}

IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = {
	## KD's Notes - There are two ways to do this, either by individually changing the variable of a colony by a set amount or by multiplying the value for all
	## KD's Notes - Due to the sheer variation between the colonies, general changes affecting all of them at once with one value would not be advised, so I haven't coded them. 6% for Angola is different than 6% for Western Sahara
	### Specific Value Addition
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { #We check if any of the temp specific value addition variables were changes. If not, then the effect shall use a multiplier is used instead
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_costs_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_costs_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_costs_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_costs_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_costs_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_costs_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_costs_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_costs_temp = 0 } }
			}
		}
		###Component Calculation
		#KD's Notes - This will be very useful for losing colonies/territories
		#It just adds the temp variables to the existing variables

		#Display Purposes First
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_angola_costs_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_mozambique_costs_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_morocco_costs_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_costs_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_costs_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_costs_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_algeria_costs_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_costs_temp }

		#Angola
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_Costs = IBR_colonial_angola_costs_temp }
		#Mozambique
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_Costs = IBR_colonial_mozambique_costs_temp }
		#Morocco
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_Costs = IBR_colonial_morocco_costs_temp }
		#Guinea-Bissau
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_Costs = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_costs_temp }
		#Sao Tome
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_Costs = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_costs_temp }
		#Cabo Verde
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_Costs = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_costs_temp }
		#Equatorial Guinea
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_Costs = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs_temp }
		#Algeria
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_Costs = IBR_colonial_algeria_costs_temp }
		#Western Sahara
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_Costs = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_costs_temp }

		#Now we add it to the overall value
		set_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = 0 } #Hard Reset to avoid overflow
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_angola_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_mozambique_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_morocco_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_algeria_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_Costs }
	}

	### Multiplier Addition
	else = {
		###Component Calculation

		##First we need to check the total change
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = 0 } #

		##KD's Notes - This looks messy but it's a good way to handle it.
		#In practice, this just checks each variable to see if they had a multiplier applied and only counts those that do when displaying the change
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_costs_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_angola_costs }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_angola_costs_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_costs_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_mozambique_costs }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_mozambique_costs_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_costs_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_costs }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_costs_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_costs_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_morocco_costs }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_morocco_costs_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_costs_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_costs }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_costs_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change }

		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_costs_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_costs }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_costs_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_costs_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_algeria_costs }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_algeria_costs_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_costs_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_costs }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_costs_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_change }
		}

		##Failsafe to prevent variable depletion
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_costs_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_costs_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_costs_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_costs_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_costs_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_costs_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_costs_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_costs_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_costs_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_costs_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_costs_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_costs_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_costs_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_costs_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_costs_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_costs_mult = 1 }
		}


		#KD's Notes - This will be very useful for losing colonies/territories
		#Angola
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_costs = IBR_colonial_angola_costs_mult }
		#Mozambique
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_costs = IBR_colonial_mozambique_costs_mult }
		#Morocco
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_costs = IBR_colonial_morocco_costs_mult }
		#Guinea-Bissau
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_costs = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_costs_mult }
		#Sao Tome
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_costs = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_costs_mult }
		#Cabo Verde
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_costs = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_costs_mult }
		#Equatorial Guinea
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs_mult }
		#Algeria
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_costs = IBR_colonial_algeria_costs_mult }
		#Western Sahara
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_costs = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_costs_mult }

		set_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_angola_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_mozambique_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_morocco_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_algeria_Costs }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_Costs }
	}


	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp < 0 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_costs_temp = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_decrease_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_colonial_misc_costs_increase_tt
	}
	IBR_run_other_colonial_effects_calculation = yes
}

IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = {
	## KD's Notes - There are two ways to do this, either by individually changing the variable of a colony by a set amount or by multiplying the value for all
	### Targeted Addition
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_revenue_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue_temp = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_temp = 0 } }
			}
		}
		###Component Calculation
		#KD's Notes - This will be very useful for losing colonies/territories

		#Display Purposes Fisrt
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_angola_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_temp }

		#Angola
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_revenue = IBR_colonial_angola_revenue_temp }
		#Mozambique
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue = IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue_temp }
		#Morocco
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue = IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue_temp }
		#Guinea-Bissau
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue_temp }
		#Sao Tome
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue_temp }
		#Cabo Verde
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue_temp }
		#Equatorial Guinea
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue_temp }
		#Algeria
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue = IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue_temp }
		#Western Sahara
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_temp }


		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_angola_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_temp }


		#Now we add it to the overall value
		set_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = 0 } #Hard Reset to avoid overflow
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_angola_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue }
	}

	### Multiplier Addition
	else = {

		###Component Calculation
		##First we need to check the total change
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = 0 } #

		##KD's Notes - This looks messy but it's a good way to handle it.
		#In practice, this just checks each variable to see if they had a multiplier applied and only counts those that do when displaying the change
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_revenue_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_angola_revenue }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_angola_revenue_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change }

		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_mult = 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue }
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_mult }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change }
		}

		##Failsafe to prevent variable depletion
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_revenue_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_revenue_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue_mult = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_mult < 1 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_mult = 1 }
		}


		#KD's Notes - This will be very useful for losing colonies/territories
		#Angola
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_angola_revenue = IBR_colonial_angola_revenue_mult }
		#Mozambique
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue = IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue_mult }
		#Morocco
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue = IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue_mult }
		#Guinea-Bissau
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue_mult }
		#Sao Tome
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue_mult }
		#Cabo Verde
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue_mult }
		#Equatorial Guinea
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue_mult }
		#Algeria
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue = IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue_mult }
		#Western Sahara
		multiply_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_mult }

		set_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_angola_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_mozambique_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_morocco_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_sao_tome_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_algeria_revenue }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var = IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp < 0 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_temp = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_colonial_misc_income_decrease_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_colonial_misc_income_increase_tt
	}
	IBR_run_other_colonial_effects_calculation = yes
}

IBR_run_other_colonial_effects_calculation = {
	#Base Calculation
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_consumer_goods_temp = IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var } #Hard Reset
	divide_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_consumer_goods_temp = 10 } #Divide by 10

	#Consumer Goods Modifier
	#KD's Notes - Now we carry over the temp from the above and modify it as needed
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_consumer_goods_temp = 2 } #Multiply by 2
	set_variable = { IBR_colonial_consumer_goods_var = IBR_colonial_consumer_goods_temp } #Hard Reset
	clamp_variable = {
		var = IBR_colonial_consumer_goods_var
		max = 0.45
		min = -0.15
	}

	#Recruitable Population Factor
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_consumer_goods_temp = -1 }
	set_variable = { IBR_colonial_garrison_var = IBR_colonial_consumer_goods_temp }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = IBR_colonial_garrison_var
		min = -0.45
		max = 0
	}

	#Deficit Calculation
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_deficit_temp = IBR_colonial_subsidies_misc_costs_var }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_deficit_temp = IBR_colonial_revenue_misc_income_var }

	#Political Power Cost Calculation
	set_variable = { IBR_colonial_political_var = IBR_colonial_deficit_temp }
	divide_variable = { IBR_colonial_political_var = 2 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_deficit_temp > 0 } }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = IBR_colonial_political_var
			min = 0
			max = 0.5
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_colonial_political_gain_var = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_colonial_deficit_temp = 0 } }
		set_variable = { IBR_colonial_political_gain_var = 0.10 }
		set_variable = { IBR_colonial_political_var = 0 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_colonial_political_gain_var = 0.2 }
		set_variable = { IBR_colonial_political_var = 0 }
	}

	#SocDev Effects
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_deficit_temp = -8 }
	set_variable = { IBR_colonial_deficit_var = IBR_colonial_deficit_temp }
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_multiply_deficit = IBR_colonial_political_var }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_multiply_deficit = 2 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_deficit_temp = IBR_colonial_multiply_deficit }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = IBR_colonial_deficit_var
		min = -6.5
		max = 2
	}
}
IBR_foreign_misc_costs_change = {
	add_to_variable = { IBR_foreign_investment_misc_costs_var = IBR_foreign_misc_costs_temp }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_foreign_misc_costs_temp < 0 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_foreign_misc_costs_temp = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_foreign_misc_costs_decrease_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_foreign_misc_costs_increase_tt
	}
}

### Terrorism Scripted Effects
IBR_AAS_initialize = {
	add_to_variable = { IBR_AAS_Resources = 0 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity = 5 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_Resources = 3 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_Support = 2 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_BTA_Activity = 2 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_BTA_Resources = 2 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_BTA_Support = 3 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_ETA_Activity = 4 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_ETA_Resources = 5 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_ETA_Support = 5 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Activity = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Resources = 4 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Support = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_AAS_offensive_decision_ticker = -1 }
	set_country_flag = IBR_Terrorism_GUI
	set_country_flag = country_has_gui
	set_country_flag = gui_alert_visible
	set_country_flag = gui_alert_visible_2
	hidden_effect = {
		IBR_terror_vulnerable_check = yes
		if = { # REMOVED TERRORISM FOR AI FOR TIME BEING TO SEE WHAT HAPPENS
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			country_event = { #ticker
				id = IBR_terror.505
				days = 62
			}
		}
	}
}

IBR_AAS_resources_change = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_CNT_Broken
					has_country_flag = IBR_ETA_Broken
					has_country_flag = IBR_FSLP_Broken
					has_country_flag = IBR_BTA_Broken
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_AAS_Resources = IBR_AAS_resources_temp }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_AAS_Resources min = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp > 3.99 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_add_aas_big_on_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp > 0 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_add_aas_on_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp > -4 }
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = -1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_sub_aas_on_tt
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = -1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_sub_aas_big_on_tt
		}
		IBR_AAS_resources_alert_activate = yes
	}
}

IBR_CNT_activity_change = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_CNT_Broken }
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity = IBR_CNT_activity_temp }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_CNT_Activity min = 0 max = 10 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_temp > 2.99 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_add_cnt_activity_big_on_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_temp > 1.99 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_add_cnt_activity_mid_on_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_temp > 0 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_add_cnt_activity_small_on_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_temp > -2 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_sub_cnt_activity_small_on_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_temp > -3 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_sub_cnt_activity_mid_on_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_sub_cnt_activity_big_on_tt
		}
		IBR_terror_vulnerable_check = yes
	}
}

			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_add_eta_supplies_big_on_tt

IBR_CNT_CrackdownButton_click_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity > 3 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity = -1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity > 2.4 }
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity = 2 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity = -0.4 }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = IBR_CNT_Activity min = 0 max = 10 }
	IBR_terror_vulnerable_check = yes
}

IBR_CNT_PropagandaButton_click_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Support > 3 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_Support = -1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Support > 2.16 }
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_CNT_Support = 2 }

	}
	clamp_variable = { var = IBR_CNT_Support min = 0 max = 10 }

}

			check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Support > 3 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_ETA_Support = -1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Support > 2.12 }
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_ETA_Support = 2 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { IBR_ETA_Support = -0.12 }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = IBR_ETA_Support min = 0 max = 10 }
	IBR_terror_vulnerable_check = yes
}

IBR_ETA_RaidButton_click_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Resources > 3 }

			check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Resources > 2.3 }

IBR_terror_vulnerable_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_CNT_Broken
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_CNT_vulnerable }
				check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Resources < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Support < 0.01 }
			}
			set_country_flag = IBR_CNT_vulnerable
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_CNT_vulnerable_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_CNT_vulnerable
				OR = {
					check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity > 0 }
					check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Resources > 0 }
					check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Support > 0 }
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = IBR_CNT_vulnerable
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_ETA_Broken
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_ETA_vulnerable }
				check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Activity < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Resources < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Support < 0.01 }
			}
			set_country_flag = IBR_ETA_vulnerable
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_ETA_vulnerable_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_ETA_vulnerable
				OR = {
					check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Activity > 0 }
					check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Resources > 0 }
					check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Support > 0 }
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = IBR_ETA_vulnerable
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_FSLP_Broken
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_FSLP_vulnerable }
				check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Activity < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Resources < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Support < 0.01 }
			}
			set_country_flag = IBR_FSLP_vulnerable
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_FSLP_vulnerable_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_FSLP_vulnerable
				OR = {
					check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Activity > 0 }
					check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Resources > 0 }
					check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Support > 0 }
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = IBR_FSLP_vulnerable
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_BTA_Broken
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_BTA_vulnerable }
				check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Activity < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Resources < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Support < 0.01 }
			}
			set_country_flag = IBR_BTA_vulnerable
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_BTA_vulnerable_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_BTA_vulnerable
				OR = {
					check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Activity > 0 }
					check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Resources > 0 }
					check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Support > 0 }
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = IBR_BTA_vulnerable
		}
	}

	####THE MONSTROSITY OF FINDING HOW MUCH IT COSTS TO BUY DOWN A TERROR GROUP ENTIRELY

	##Cost to buy down CNT

	#Temp variable, CNT activity

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN = IBR_CNT_Activity }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN = 0.4 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN = IBR_CNT_Activity }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN = 5 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	#Temp variable, CNT supplies

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Resources < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Resources < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN = IBR_CNT_Resources }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN = 0.4 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}

		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 2 }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN = IBR_CNT_Resources }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN = 5 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}

		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 2 }
	}

	#Temp variable, CNT support

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Support < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_Support < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN = IBR_CNT_Support }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN = 0.16 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN = IBR_CNT_Support }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN = 13 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}

	set_variable = { IBR_CNT_BUYDOWN = 5 } #ADDED 5 FOR TIME BEING, MAYBE CHANGE THIS LATER IF ITS WEIRD
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_BUYDOWN = IBR_CNT_activity_BUYDOWN_round }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_BUYDOWN = IBR_CNT_supplies_BUYDOWN_round }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_BUYDOWN = IBR_CNT_support_BUYDOWN_round }

	##Cost to buy down FSLP

	#Temp variable, FSLP activity

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Activity < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Activity < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN = IBR_FSLP_Activity }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN = 0.4 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN = IBR_FSLP_Activity }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN = 5 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN_round
	}
	#Temp variable, FSLP supplies

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Resources < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Resources < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN = IBR_FSLP_Resources }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN = 0.4 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}

		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 2 }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN = IBR_FSLP_Resources }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN = 5 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}

		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 2 }
	}

	#Temp variable, FSLP support

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Support < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_Support < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN = IBR_FSLP_Support }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN = 0.16 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN = IBR_FSLP_Support }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN = 13 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}

	set_variable = { IBR_FSLP_BUYDOWN = 5 } #ADDED 5 FOR TIME BEING, MAYBE CHANGE THIS LATER IF ITS WEIRD
	add_to_variable = { IBR_FSLP_BUYDOWN = IBR_FSLP_activity_BUYDOWN_round }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_FSLP_BUYDOWN = IBR_FSLP_supplies_BUYDOWN_round }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_FSLP_BUYDOWN = IBR_FSLP_support_BUYDOWN_round }

	##Cost to buy down ETA

	#Temp variable, ETA activity

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Activity < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Activity < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN = IBR_ETA_Activity }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN = 0.3 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN = IBR_ETA_Activity }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN = 7 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	#Temp variable, ETA supplies

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Resources < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Resources < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN = IBR_ETA_Resources }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN = 0.3 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}

		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 2 }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN = IBR_ETA_Resources }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN = 7 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}

		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 2 }
	}

	#Temp variable, ETA support

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Support < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_Support < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN = IBR_ETA_Support }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN = 0.12 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN = IBR_ETA_Support }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN = 17 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}

	set_variable = { IBR_ETA_BUYDOWN = 5 } #ADDED 5 FOR TIME BEING, MAYBE CHANGE THIS LATER IF ITS WEIRD
	add_to_variable = { IBR_ETA_BUYDOWN = IBR_ETA_activity_BUYDOWN_round }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_ETA_BUYDOWN = IBR_ETA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_ETA_BUYDOWN = IBR_ETA_support_BUYDOWN_round }

	##Cost to buy down BTA (TBA)

	#Temp variable, TBA activity

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Activity < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Activity < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN = IBR_BTA_Activity }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN = 0.5 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN = IBR_BTA_Activity }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN = 4 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	#Temp variable, TBA supplies

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Resources < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Resources < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN = IBR_BTA_Resources }

		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN = 0.5 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}

		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 2 }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN = IBR_BTA_Resources }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN = 4 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}

		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round = 2 }
	}

	#Temp variable, TBA support

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Support < 0 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN_round = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_Support < 2.1 } }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN = IBR_BTA_Support }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN = 0.2 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN = IBR_BTA_Support }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN = 2 }

		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN = 10 }

		#ROUND UP

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN_round = IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN }

		round_temp_variable = IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN_round

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN_round < IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN } }

			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN_round = 1 }
		}
	}

	set_variable = { IBR_BTA_BUYDOWN = 5 } #ADDED 5 FOR TIME BEING, MAYBE CHANGE THIS LATER IF ITS WEIRD
	add_to_variable = { IBR_BTA_BUYDOWN = IBR_BTA_activity_BUYDOWN_round }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_BTA_BUYDOWN = IBR_BTA_supplies_BUYDOWN_round }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_BTA_BUYDOWN = IBR_BTA_support_BUYDOWN_round }
}

IBR_assign_current_goverment_terrorism_multiplier = { # Checks need to be updated to current leaders with
	set_variable = { IBR_current_goverment_terrorism_mult = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = IBRAuthDemCongress
				AND = { # Dem Iberia Pre-Election Period
					has_government = paternalism
					has_country_leader = { character = IBR_Francisco_Franco ruling_only = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		multiply_variable = { IBR_current_goverment_terrorism_mult = 0.7 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = IBR_Manuel_Fraga
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
		multiply_variable = { IBR_current_goverment_terrorism_mult = 0.4 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = IBR_Joao_Miller_Guerra
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
		multiply_variable = { IBR_current_goverment_terrorism_mult = 0.1 }
	}
}

IBR_CNT_ticker = {
	set_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_gain = 0.15 }
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_gain_mult = IBR_CNT_Support }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_gain_mult = 0.33 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				check_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_gain_mult < 1 }
			}
		}
		multiply_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_gain = IBR_CNT_support_gain_mult }
	}
	multiply_variable = { IBR_CNT_support_gain = IBR_current_goverment_terrorism_mult }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_Support = IBR_CNT_support_gain }

	###For each separatist group - activity gain

	set_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_gain = 0.075 }
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_gain_add = IBR_CNT_Support }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_gain_add = 0.125 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_gain = IBR_CNT_activity_gain_add }

	multiply_variable = { IBR_CNT_activity_gain = IBR_current_goverment_terrorism_mult }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_Activity = IBR_CNT_activity_gain }

	###For each separatist group - supply gain

	set_variable = { IBR_CNT_resources_gain = 0.075 }
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_resources_gain_add = IBR_CNT_Support }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_CNT_resources_gain_add = 0.125 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_resources_gain = IBR_CNT_resources_gain_add }

	multiply_variable = { IBR_CNT_resources_gain = IBR_current_goverment_terrorism_mult }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_CNT_Resources = IBR_CNT_resources_gain }

	clamp_variable = { var = IBR_CNT_Activity min = 0 max = 10 }
	clamp_variable = { var = IBR_CNT_Resources min = 0 max = 10 }
	clamp_variable = { var = IBR_CNT_Support min = 0 max = 10 }
}

IBR_clear_terror_roller_flags = {
	clr_country_flag = IBR_CNT_Rolled
	clr_country_flag = IBR_ETA_Rolled
	clr_country_flag = IBR_BTA_Rolled
	clr_country_flag = IBR_FSLP_Rolled
}

IBR_check_remove_terrorism_gui = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_FSLP_Broken
			has_country_flag = IBR_CNT_Broken
			has_country_flag = IBR_ETA_Broken
			has_country_flag = IBR_BTA_Broken
		}
		clr_country_flag = IBR_Terrorism_GUI
		clr_country_flag = IBR_terrorism_window_open_flag
		clr_country_flag = country_has_gui
		clr_country_flag = gui_alert_visible
		clr_country_flag = gui_alert_visible_2
		set_temp_variable = { alert_id = token:Alert_AAS_Resources }
		remove_TNO_alert = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			remove_decision = IBR_AAS_Funding_decision
			remove_decision = IBR_AAS_Military_decision
			remove_decision = IBR_AAS_CNT_offensive_decision
			remove_decision = IBR_AAS_ETA_offensive_decision
			remove_decision = IBR_AAS_FSLP_offensive_decision
			remove_decision = IBR_AAS_BTA_offensive_decision
		}
	}
}

IBR_AAS_resources_ticker = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = IBRChoseCongress
				has_country_flag = IBRChoseUnion
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_goodending
					has_country_flag = IBR_Salazar_dead
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 6 }
		IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_goodending
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_midending
					has_country_flag = IBR_Salazar_dead
				}
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_badending
					has_country_flag = IBR_Salazar_dead
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 5 }
		IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_midending
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_worstending
					has_country_flag = IBR_Salazar_dead
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 4 }
		IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_start
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 2 }
		IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 1 }
		IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
	}
}

IBR_AAS_resources_alert_activate = { #fires up the alert when new resources are available
	set_temp_variable = { alert_id = token:Alert_AAS_Resources }
	if = { # Adds Alert if AAS Resources Increase
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp > 0 }
				check_variable = { IBR_AAS_Resources > 0 }
				has_country_flag = IBR_Terrorism_GUI
			}
		}
		add_TNO_alert_unless_exists = yes
	}
	else_if = { # Removes Alert if Resources Hit Zero
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_AAS_Resources = 0 }
		}
		remove_TNO_alert_data = yes
	}
}

IBR_clear_AAS_chain_flags = { #put this at start of 1970 paths
	clr_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_start
	clr_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_goodending
	clr_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_midending
	clr_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_badending
	clr_country_flag = IBR_AAS_chain_worstending
}

IBR_check_caudillo_popularity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_caudillo_mechanic_over
			}
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_military_alignment min = -20 max = 20 }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_church_alignment min = -20 max = 20 }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_business_alignment min = -15 max = 15 }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_intellectual_alignment min = -15 max = 15 }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_foreign_alignment min = -15 max = 15 }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_bureaucrat_alignment min = -15 max = 15 }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_settlers_alignment min = -10 max = 10 }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_native_alignment min = -5 max = 5 }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_majority_alignment min = -10 max = 10 }
		clamp_variable = { var = IBR_regional_alignment min = -10 max = 10 }

		set_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = 0 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_military_alignment }
		add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_church_alignment }
		add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_bureaucrat_alignment }
		add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_business_alignment }
		add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_settlers_alignment }
		add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_native_alignment }
		add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_foreign_alignment }
		add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_intellectual_alignment }
		add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_majority_alignment }
		add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_regional_alignment }

		# There has to be a better way to add the temp variable for the tooltip than this but I'm not figuring it out - Rune
		set_variable = { IBR_check_caudillo_actual = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_check_caudillo_actual = 0 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable = IBR_alignment_change_sum }
		}
		clear_variable = IBR_check_caudillo_actual

		#log = "Caudillo Popularity Score: [?caudillo_temp_variable]" #For testing purposes

		if = { # Nothing Updates while being used by Rogue Council + Pre-Election mechanics
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_rogue
					has_country_flag = IBRDemUnion
				}
			}
			if = { # Post-Salazar Interim
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_Salazar_transition
				}
				if = { # Strong Franco, [30,+∞)
					limit = {
						check_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable > 29.99 }
					}
					clr_country_flag = cau_weak_franco
					clr_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
					clr_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
					set_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
				}
				else_if = { # Weak Franco, (0,30)
					limit = {
						check_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable > 0 }
					}
					clr_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
					clr_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
					clr_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
					set_country_flag = cau_weak_franco
				}
				else_if = { # Weak Salazar, (-30,0]
					limit = {
						check_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable > -30 }
					}
					clr_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
					clr_country_flag = cau_weak_franco
					clr_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
					set_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
				}
				else_if = { # Strong Salazar, (-∞,-30]
					limit = {
						check_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable < -29.99 }
					}
					clr_country_flag = cau_weak_franco
					clr_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
					clr_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
					set_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Strong Franco, [30,+∞)
				limit = {
					check_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable > 29.99 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
							has_country_flag = cau_weak_franco
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = cau_weak_franco_tt
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = cau_strong_franco_tt
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					IBR_Francisco_Franco = {
						promote_character = despotism_civilian_dictatorship_subtype
						promote_character = fascism_2_subtype
						promote_character = fascism_subtype
						promote_character = despotism_3_subtype
						promote_character = despotism_2_subtype
						promote_character = paternalism_subtype
						promote_character = paternalism_1_subtype
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_leader_with_trait = weak_franco_trait
						}
						remove_country_leader_trait = weak_franco_trait
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_country_leader_with_trait = strong_franco_trait }
						}
						add_country_leader_trait = strong_franco_trait
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = IBR_caetano_path }
						add_ideas = IBR_Marcelo_Caetano_hog
						remove_ideas = IBR_Francisco_Franco_Post_Salazar_hog
					}
					else = {
						add_ideas = IBR_Antonio_Salazar_hog
						remove_ideas = IBR_Francisco_Franco_hog
					}
				}
				clr_country_flag = cau_weak_franco
				clr_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
				clr_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
				set_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
			}
			else_if = { # Weak Franco, (0,30)
				limit = {
					check_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable > 0 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = cau_weakened_franco_tt
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_flag = cau_weak_franco
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = cau_weak_franco_tt
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					IBR_Francisco_Franco = {
						promote_character = despotism_civilian_dictatorship_subtype
						promote_character = fascism_2_subtype
						promote_character = fascism_subtype
						promote_character = despotism_3_subtype
						promote_character = despotism_2_subtype
						promote_character = paternalism_subtype
						promote_character = paternalism_1_subtype
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_leader_with_trait = strong_franco_trait
						}
						remove_country_leader_trait = strong_franco_trait
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_country_leader_with_trait = weak_franco_trait }
						}
						add_country_leader_trait = weak_franco_trait
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = IBR_caetano_path }
						add_ideas = IBR_Marcelo_Caetano_hog
						remove_ideas = IBR_Francisco_Franco_Post_Salazar_hog
					}
					else = {
						add_ideas = IBR_Antonio_Salazar_hog
						remove_ideas = IBR_Francisco_Franco_hog
					}
				}
				clr_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
				clr_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
				clr_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
				set_country_flag = cau_weak_franco
			}
			else_if = { # Weak Salazar, (-30,0]
				limit = {
					check_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable > -30 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = cau_weakened_salazar_tt
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = cau_weak_salazar_tt
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					IBR = {
						if = {
							limit = { has_country_flag = IBR_caetano_path }
							IBR_Marcelo_Caetano = {
								promote_character = despotism_civilian_dictatorship_subtype
								promote_character = fascism_2_subtype
								promote_character = fascism_subtype
								promote_character = despotism_3_subtype
								promote_character = despotism_2_subtype
								promote_character = paternalism_subtype
								promote_character = paternalism_1_subtype
							}
							remove_ideas = IBR_Marcelo_Caetano_hog
							add_ideas = IBR_Francisco_Franco_Post_Salazar_hog
						}
						else = {
							IBR_Antonio_Salazar = {
								promote_character = despotism_civilian_dictatorship_subtype
								promote_character = fascism_2_subtype
								promote_character = fascism_subtype
								promote_character = despotism_3_subtype
								promote_character = despotism_2_subtype
								promote_character = paternalism_subtype
								promote_character = paternalism_1_subtype
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_country_leader_with_trait = strong_salazar_trait
								}
								remove_country_leader_trait = strong_salazar_trait
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									NOT = { has_country_leader_with_trait = weak_salazar_trait }
								}
								add_country_leader_trait = weak_salazar_trait
							}
							remove_ideas = IBR_Antonio_Salazar_hog
							add_ideas = IBR_Francisco_Franco_hog
						}
					}
				}
				clr_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
				clr_country_flag = cau_weak_franco
				clr_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
				set_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
			}
			else_if = { # Strong Salazar, (-∞,-30]
				limit = {
					check_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable < -29.99 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
							has_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = cau_weak_salazar_tt
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = cau_strong_salazar_tt
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					IBR = {
						if = {
							limit = { has_country_flag = IBR_caetano_path }
							IBR_Marcelo_Caetano = {
								promote_character = despotism_civilian_dictatorship_subtype
								promote_character = fascism_2_subtype
								promote_character = fascism_subtype

						else = {

								promote_character = despotism_civilian_dictatorship_subtype
								promote_character = fascism_2_subtype
								promote_character = fascism_subtype
								promote_character = despotism_3_subtype
								promote_character = despotism_2_subtype

				clr_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
				clr_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
				set_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
			}
			IBR_update_caudillo_progress_bar = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable > 0 }
				}
				clr_country_flag = cau_salazar_lead
				set_country_flag = cau_franco_lead
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { caudillo_temp_variable < 0 }
				}
				clr_country_flag = cau_franco_lead
				set_country_flag = cau_salazar_lead
			}
			else = {
				clr_country_flag = cau_salazar_lead
				clr_country_flag = cau_franco_lead
			}
		}
	}

IBR_Compute_Duet_Balancing = {
	### Franco's Actions
	custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_duet_franco_actions_tt
	#Military
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_military_alignment < -9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_military_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_military_alignment < -4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 70
			IBR_improve_military_opinion_franco = yes
		}
	}
	#Bureaucrats
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_bureaucrat_alignment < -9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_bureaucrats_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_bureaucrat_alignment < -4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 40
			IBR_improve_bureaucrats_opinion_franco = yes
		}
	}
	#Church
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_church_alignment < -9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_church_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_church_alignment < -4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 50
			IBR_improve_church_opinion_franco = yes
		}
	}
	#Businessmen
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_business_alignment < -9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_business_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_business_alignment < -4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 40
			IBR_improve_business_opinion_franco = yes
		}
	}
	#Intellectuals
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_intellectual_alignment < -9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_intellectual_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_intellectual_alignment < -4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 35
			IBR_improve_intellectual_opinion_franco = yes
		}
	}
	#Colonial Settlers
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_settlers_alignment < -9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_settlers_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_settlers_alignment < -4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 40
			IBR_improve_settlers_opinion_franco = yes
		}
	}
	#Colonial Native
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_native_alignment < -4.99 } }
		IBR_improve_native_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	#Foreign Leaders
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_foreign_alignment < -9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_foreign_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_foreign_alignment < -4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 50
			IBR_improve_foreign_opinion_franco = yes
		}
	}
	#Silent Majority
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_majority_alignment < -9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_majority_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_majority_alignment < -4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 50
			IBR_improve_majority_opinion_franco = yes
		}
	}
	#Regionalist Bourgouise
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_regional_alignment < -9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_regional_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_regional_alignment < -4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 50
			IBR_improve_regional_opinion_franco = yes
		}
	}

	### Salazar's Actions
	custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_duet_salazar_actions_tt
	#Military
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_military_alignment > 9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_military_opinion_salazar = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_military_alignment > 4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 30
			IBR_improve_military_opinion_salazar = yes
		}
	}
	#Bureaucrats
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_bureaucrat_alignment > 9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_bureaucrats_opinion_salazar = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_bureaucrat_alignment > 4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 55
			IBR_improve_bureaucrats_opinion_salazar = yes
		}
	}
	#Church
	if = {

		random = {
			chance = 60
			IBR_improve_church_opinion_salazar = yes
		}
	}
	#Businessmen
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_business_alignment > 9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_business_opinion_salazar = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_business_alignment > 4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 60
			IBR_improve_business_opinion_salazar = yes
		}
	}
	#Intellectuals
	if = {

		IBR_improve_intellectual_opinion_salazar = yes
	}

	#Colonial Settlers
	if = {

		IBR_improve_settlers_opinion_salazar = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_settlers_alignment > 4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 60
			IBR_improve_settlers_opinion_salazar = yes
		}
	}
	#Colonial Native
	if = {

		IBR_improve_native_opinion_salazar = yes
	}
	#Foreign Leaders
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_foreign_alignment > 9.99 } }
		IBR_improve_foreign_opinion_salazar = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_foreign_alignment > 4.99 } }
		random = {
			chance = 50
			IBR_improve_foreign_opinion_salazar = yes
		}
	}
	#Silent Majority
	if = {

	if = {

IBR_Compute_Duet_Balancing_Caetano = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_duet_not_solo_caetano_addendum_tt
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = cau_strong_franco }
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_springboard_temp = -2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = cau_weak_franco }
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_springboard_temp = -1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar }
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_springboard_temp = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar }
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_springboard_temp = 2 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence_temp = IBR_springboard_temp }
	IBR_caetano_influence_change = yes
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence_temp = IBR_springboard_temp }
	IBR_choupana_influence_change = yes
}
#Fate of the Council - Rogue council vs. Caudillos mechanic

IBR_Rogue_Council_setup = {
	remove_mission = IBR_duet_not_solo
	set_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_rogue
	#change GUI with this flag?
	#switch out decisions with this flag

	#Starting value of the ticker in days (reformism x 1.6 -> e.g. 100 reformism = 160 days)
	#vs 10x21 day foci = 210 days

	set_variable = { IBR_Rogue_Council_days = Iberian_Council_reformism_var }
	multiply_variable = { IBR_Rogue_Council_days = 1.6 }
	set_variable = { IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar_saved_value = IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar }
	set_variable = { IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar = 25 }
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_rogue_council_influence_temp_var = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { political_power > 150 }
		}
		set_political_power = 150
	}
	IBR_Rogue_Council_modify_council_influence = yes
	activate_mission = IBR_rogue_council_mission
	activate_mission = IBR_caudillo_check_mission
	#
}

#Fate of the Council - Add/Subtract days to the mission

IBR_Rogue_Council_modify_mission_days = {
	hidden_effect = {
		add_days_mission_timeout = {
			mission = IBR_rogue_council_mission
			days = IBR_rogue_council_mission_days_temp_var
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_rogue_council_mission_days_temp_var > 0 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_Rogue_Council_modify_mission_days_positive_tt
	}
	else = {
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_rogue_council_mission_days_temp_var = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_Rogue_Council_modify_mission_days_negative_tt
	}
}

#Fate of the Council - Add/Subtract council influence & calculate weekly loss

IBR_Rogue_Council_modify_council_influence = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar_temp = IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar_temp = IBR_rogue_council_influence_temp_var }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar_temp
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar = IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar_temp }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_var_caudillo_days_calc = 50 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { temp_var_caudillo_days_calc = IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar_temp }
		divide_temp_variable = { temp_var_caudillo_days_calc = 10 }
		round_temp_variable = temp_var_caudillo_days_calc
		set_variable = { IBR_rogue_council_weekly_loss = temp_var_caudillo_days_calc }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_rogue_council_influence_temp_var > 0 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_Rogue_Council_modify_council_influence_positive_tt
	}
	else = {
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_rogue_council_influence_temp_var = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_Rogue_Council_modify_council_influence_negative_tt
	}
	# This tooltip is scripted loc because round_temp_variable is an abomination that works for the tooltip there, but not here
	custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_Rogue_Council_modify_caudillo_days_effect_tt
}

IBR_remove_all_ideas = {
	remove_ideas = IBR_Iberian_Economic_Nightmare
	remove_ideas = IBR_Betray_Old_Guard
	remove_ideas = IBR_Crippled_Sovereignty
	remove_ideas = IBR_Crippled_Sovereignty_notsomuch
	remove_ideas = IBR_liberalisation
	remove_ideas = IBR_Disorderly_Federal_Army
	remove_ideas = IBR_Disorderly_Federal_Army_2
}

IBRWarsAddPORclaims = {
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			is_portuguese_state = yes
		}
