IBR_divorce_generals = {
	every_unit_leader = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				# Fied Marshals
				is_character = IBR_Agustin_Munoz_Grandes
				# Generals
				is_character = IBR_Carlos_Asensio_Cabanillas
				is_character = IBR_Francisco_Coloma_Gallegos
				is_character = IBR_Mohammed_Mezziane
				is_character = IBR_Alfonso_Armada
				is_character = IBR_Rafael_Garcia_Valino
				is_character = IBR_Manuel_Diez_Alegria
				is_character = IBR_Camilo_Alonso_Vega
				is_character = IBR_Santiago_Amado_Loriga
				# Admirals
				is_character = IBR_Luis_Carrero_Blanco
				is_character = IBR_Angel_Liberal_Lucini
				is_character = IBR_Gabriel_Pita_Veiga
				is_character = IBR_Adolfo_Baturone_Colombo
				is_character = IBR_Pedro_Nieto_Antunez

			}
		}
		set_nationality = SPA
	}
	every_unit_leader = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				# Generals
				is_character = IBR_Antonio_Spinola
				is_character = IBR_Francisco_Costa_Gomes
				is_character = IBR_Afonso_Almeida_Fernandes
				# Admirals
				is_character = IBR_Americo_Tomas
				is_character = IBR_Henrique_Tenreiro
			}
		}
		set_nationality = POR
	}
}

IBR_improve_military_opinion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = IBR_Francisco_Franco
			}
		}
		IBR_improve_military_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
				has_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
			}
		}
		IBR_improve_military_opinion_salazar = yes
	}
	else = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					IBR_improve_military_opinion_franco = yes
				}
				50 = {
					IBR_improve_military_opinion_salazar = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
IBR_improve_military_opinion_franco = {
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_military_alignment_temp = 2 }
	IBR_change_military_opinion_franco = yes
}
IBR_improve_military_opinion_salazar = {
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_military_alignment_temp = -2 }
	IBR_change_military_opinion_salazar = yes
}

					IBR_worsen_foreign_opinion_salazar = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
IBR_worsen_foreign_opinion_franco = {
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_foreign_alignment_temp = -1.5 }
	IBR_change_foreign_opinion_franco = yes
}
IBR_worsen_foreign_opinion_salazar = {
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_foreign_alignment_temp = 1.5 }
	IBR_change_foreign_opinion_salazar = yes
}

IBR_worsen_intellectual_opinion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = IBR_Francisco_Franco
			}
		}
		IBR_worsen_intellectual_opinion_franco = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
				has_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
			}
		}
		IBR_worsen_intellectual_opinion_salazar = yes
	}
	else = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					IBR_worsen_intellectual_opinion_franco = yes
				}
				50 = {
					IBR_worsen_intellectual_opinion_salazar = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Both use - for Salazar and + for Franco leaning, difference is tooltipping.
IBR_change_military_opinion_franco = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_rogue
				has_country_flag = IBRDemUnion
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_military_alignment = IBR_military_alignment_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_alignment_change_sum = IBR_military_alignment_temp }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_military_alignment_temp > 0 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_improve_franco_army_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_worsen_franco_army_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = { IBR_check_caudillo_popularity = yes }
	}
}
IBR_change_military_opinion_salazar = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {

IBR_council_clamp = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag }

		clamp_variable = {
			var = Iberian_Council_influence_var
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
	}
	else = {
		clamp_variable = {
			var = Iberian_Council_reformism_var
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
	}
}

IBR_update_caudillo_progress_bar = {
	set_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = 0 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = IBR_military_alignment }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = IBR_church_alignment }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = IBR_bureaucrat_alignment }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = IBR_business_alignment }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = IBR_settlers_alignment }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = IBR_native_alignment }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = IBR_foreign_alignment }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = IBR_intellectual_alignment }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = IBR_majority_alignment }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = IBR_regional_alignment }

	# Total Alignment is any direction is 135, so multiplier of 2.7 uses full bar for full alignment
	divide_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = 2.7 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caudillo_temp_bar_variable = 50 }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = caudillo_temp_bar_variable
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	set_variable = { IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar = caudillo_temp_bar_variable }
}

#lisbon flood
IBR_lisbon_coverup_check = {
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_reliefx5 = IBR_lisbonflood_relief }	# Min of 1, Max of 8
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_reliefx5 = 5 }					# Min of 5, Max of 40
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_coverup = IBR_lisbonflood_censor }	# Max of 5 - Needs to match min of previous to prevent negative value in random list
	divide_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_coverup = IBR_lisbonflood_reliefx5 }
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_coverup_bypass = 1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_coverup_bypass = IBR_lisbonflood_coverup }

	hidden_effect = {
		random_list = {
			IBR_lisbonflood_coverup = {
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_lisbon_flood.10
					days = 7
				}
			}
			IBR_lisbonflood_coverup_bypass = {
				IBR_lisbon_support_check = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

IBR_lisbon_support_check = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_support < 4 }
				check_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_censor < 3 }
			}
			country_event = {
				id = IBR_lisbon_flood.11
				days = 1
			}
		}
		else = {
			IBR_lisbon_outcome_check = yes
		}
	}
}

IBR_lisbon_outcome_check = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_relief < 4 }
			}
			country_event = {
				id = IBR_lisbon_flood.14
				days = 11
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_relief < 7 }
			}
			country_event = {
				id = IBR_lisbon_flood.13
				days = 11
			}
		}
		else = {
			country_event = {
				id = IBR_lisbon_flood.12
				days = 11
			}
		}
	}
}

IBR_lisbon_loss_calc = {
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_losscalc_1000 = 1000 }
	divide_temp_variable = { IBR_losscalc_1000 = IBR_lisbonflood_relief } # Relief as max of 8, so min is 125
	add_to_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_loss = IBR_losscalc_1000 } # Min is 325
	round_variable = IBR_lisbonflood_loss
}

IBR_lisbon_anger_calc = {
	set_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_anger = IBR_lisbonflood_support } # Min of 0, Max of 10
	subtract_from_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_anger = 8 } # Min of -8, Max of 2

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_C = IBR_lisbonflood_censor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_C = 1 }
	divide_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_C = 2 }

	divide_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_anger = IBR_lisbonflood_C } # Max of 2/3

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_proploss = IBR_lisbonflood_loss }
	divide_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_proploss = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_anger = IBR_lisbon_proploss }
}

IBR_lisbon_final_effects = {
	## Manpower

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_manpower = IBR_lisbonflood_loss }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_manpower = -0.25 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_lisbon_manpower
	add_manpower = IBR_lisbon_manpower

	## Council Reformism/Influnece

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_council = IBR_lisbonflood_anger }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_council = -0.3 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_created_flag
			check_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_anger < -2.9 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { Iberian_Council_reformism_var = IBR_lisbon_council }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_reformist_tt_lisbonflood
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag
			check_variable = { IBR_lisbonflood_anger < -2.9 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { Iberian_Council_influence_var = IBR_lisbon_council }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_influence_tt_lisbonflood
	}
	IBR_council_clamp = yes

	## Stability

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_stability = IBR_lisbonflood_anger }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_stability = 3.25 }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_stability_temp = IBR_lisbon_stability }
	IBR_stability_change = yes

	## FSLP Variables

	#FSLP Support
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_FSLP_support = IBR_lisbonflood_anger }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_FSLP_support = -0.3 }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_support_temp = IBR_lisbon_FSLP_support }
	IBR_FSLP_support_change = yes

	#FSLP Activity
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_FSLP_activity = IBR_lisbonflood_anger }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_lisbon_FSLP_activity = -0.2 }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_FSLP_activity_temp = IBR_lisbon_FSLP_activity }
	IBR_FSLP_activity_change = yes

	##GDP (Tbh i'll lay off gdp for this.. i feel it already has enough effects to be honest)

	# Wipes Flood Vars
	clear_variable = IBR_lisbonflood_relief
	clear_variable = IBR_lisbonflood_censor
	clear_variable = IBR_lisbonflood_support
	clear_variable = IBR_lisbonflood_loss
	clear_variable = IBR_lisbonflood_anger
}

IBR_iberian_divorce_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				SLG = {
					is_subject_of = IBR
				}
			}
			SLG = {
				transfer_state = 1006
				transfer_state = 459
				transfer_state = 2118
				set_cosmetic_tag = SLG_FREE_YVES
			}
			IBR = {
				end_puppet = SLG
			}
		}
		IBR_divorce_generals = yes
		SPA = { inherit_technology = IBR }
		POR = { inherit_technology = IBR }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = IBR }
			remove_core_of = IBR
			add_core_of = SPA
		}
		SPA = {
			annex_country = {
				target = IBR
				transfer_troops = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { IBR = { has_country_flag = IBR_Salazar_dead } }
			POR = {
				kill_country_leader = yes
				add_country_leader_role = {
					character = POR_Pedro_Pereira
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						desc = "POLITICS_PEDRO_PEREIRA_DESC"
						expire = "1981.10.20"
						ideology = despotism_civilian_dictatorship_subtype
						traits = { }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		SPA = {
			load_oob = "SPA_1962"
			set_temp_variable = { TNO_economy_subtype_temp = token:Econ_Subtype_Dirigisme }
			econ_subtype_change = yes
		}
		POR = {
			transfer_state = 112
			transfer_state = 179
			transfer_state = 180
			transfer_state = 181
			transfer_state = 997
			transfer_state = 998
			transfer_state = 1000
			transfer_state = 1004
			transfer_state = 296
			transfer_state = 705
			transfer_state = 1672
			transfer_state = 1673
			transfer_state = 1674
			transfer_state = 698
			transfer_state = 702
			transfer_state = 697
			every_owned_state = {
				remove_core_of = SPA
				add_core_of = POR
			}
			load_oob = "POR_1962"
			set_temp_variable = { TNO_economy_subtype_temp = token:Econ_Subtype_Dirigisme }
			econ_subtype_change = yes
		}
		SPA = { TNO_startup_nation = yes }
		POR = { TNO_startup_nation = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = IBRStockpilesSpainBias }
			transfer_units_fraction = {
				target = SPA
				size = 0
				stockpile_ratio = 0.7
			}
			transfer_units_fraction = {
				target = POR
				size = 0
				stockpile_ratio = 1.0
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = IBRStockpilesPortugalBias }
			transfer_units_fraction = {
				target = SPA
				size = 0
				stockpile_ratio = 0.3
			}
			transfer_units_fraction = {
				target = POR
				size = 0
				stockpile_ratio = 1.0
			}
		}
		else = {
			transfer_units_fraction = {
				target = SPA
				size = 0
				stockpile_ratio = 0.5
			}
			transfer_units_fraction = {
				target = POR
				size = 0
				stockpile_ratio = 1.0
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = IBROfficersSpainBias }
			SPA = {
				army_experience = 40
				navy_experience = 40
				air_experience = 40
			}
			POR = {
				army_experience = 10
				navy_experience = 10
				air_experience = 10
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = IBROfficersPortugalBias }
			SPA = {
				army_experience = 10
				navy_experience = 10
				air_experience = 10
			}
			POR = {
				army_experience = 40
				navy_experience = 40
				air_experience = 40
			}
		}
		else = {
			SPA = {
				army_experience = 25
				navy_experience = 25
				air_experience = 25
			}
			POR = {
				army_experience = 25
				navy_experience = 25
				air_experience = 25
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = IBRPortLeasingPTS }
			SPA = {
				diplomatic_relation = {
					country = POR
					relation = docking_rights
					active = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = IBRPortLeasingSTP }
			POR = {
				diplomatic_relation = {
					country = SPA
					relation = docking_rights
					active = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { SPA_GDP_growth_temp = 2.5 }
		set_temp_variable = { POR_GDP_growth_temp = 2.5 }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_economic_balance_temp = 0.7 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_economic_balance_temp = IBREconomicBalance }
		add_to_temp_variable = { SPA_GDP_growth_temp = IBR_economic_balance_temp }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { POR_GDP_growth_temp = IBR_economic_balance_temp }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_economic_strength_temp = 0.5 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_economic_strength_temp = IBREconomicStrength }
		add_to_temp_variable = { SPA_GDP_growth_temp = IBR_economic_strength_temp }
		add_to_temp_variable = { POR_GDP_growth_temp = IBR_economic_strength_temp }

		add_to_variable = { SPA.GDP_growth = SPA_GDP_growth_temp }
		add_to_variable = { POR.GDP_growth = POR_GDP_growth_temp }

		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				HasIberiaInterest = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = WORLD.491
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { MOR = { has_country_flag = MOR_IBR_puppet } }
			MOR = { save_party_popularities = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { MOR = { has_country_flag = MOR_associate } }
				SPA = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = MOR
						autonomous_state = autonomy_associated
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				SPA = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = MOR
						autonomous_state = autonomy_reliant
					}
				}
			}
			MOR = { apply_party_popularities = yes }
		}
		set_global_flag = Iberian_Divorce
	}
}

IBR_economy_recovery_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_Recovery_Progress > 11 }
		}
		country_event = { # Successful Recovery
			id = IBR_post_trium.30
			days = 2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_Recovery_Progress > 6 }
		}
		country_event = { # Partial Recovery
			id = IBR_post_trium.29
			days = 2
		}
	}
	else = {
		country_event = { # Failed Recovery
			id = IBR_post_trium.28
			days = 2
		}
	}
}

#Caetano-Choupana Power Struggle
IBR_caetano_springboard_setup = {
	set_country_flag = IBR_caetano_springboard
	custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_for_the_good_of_the_union_tt
	set_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence = 10 }
	set_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence = 10 }
	hidden_effect = {
		activate_mission = IBR_caetano_springboard_category_mission
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = paternalism_1
			popularity = 0.1
		}
		set_variable = { paternalism_1_var = token:IBR_paternalism_1_auth }
		IBR_Francisco_Franco = {
			promote_character = paternalism_1_subtype
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_caetano_springboard_stab_var = -0.2 }
		set_variable = { IBR_caetano_springboard_pp_var = -0.2 }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = IBR_caetano_springboard_dyn_mod }
	}
	IBR_caetano_springboard_calculation = yes
}
IBR_caetano_springboard_disable = {
	clr_country_flag = IBR_caetano_springboard
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_mission = IBR_caetano_springboard_category_mission
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = IBR_caetano_springboard_dyn_mod }
	}
}
IBR_caetano_springboard_reenable = { # Rogue Council Failure
	set_country_flag = IBR_caetano_springboard
	hidden_effect = {
		activate_mission = IBR_caetano_springboard_category_mission
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = IBR_caetano_springboard_dyn_mod }
	}
	IBR_caetano_springboard_calculation = yes
}
IBR_caetano_springboard_calculation = {
	# Compute start and end values
	# Japanese is first so its start is 0 and end is just its %
	set_temp_variable = { spain_1_st = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { spain_1_end = 100 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { spain_1_end = IBR_caetano_influence }

	# Compute frame = SSSEEE by shifting over the start number 3 places
	# and adding the end number
	set_variable = { IBR_spain_1_frame = spain_1_st }
	round_variable = IBR_spain_1_frame
	multiply_variable = { IBR_spain_1_frame = 1000 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_spain_1_frame = spain_1_end }

	# Compute start and end values
	# caetano starts where spain_1 ends
	set_temp_variable = { caetano_st = spain_1_end }
	set_temp_variable = { caetano_end = caetano_st }
	add_to_temp_variable = { caetano_end = 100 }

	set_variable = { IBR_caetano_frame = caetano_st }
	round_variable = IBR_caetano_frame
	multiply_variable = { IBR_caetano_frame = 1000 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_caetano_frame = caetano_end }

	### choupana piechart

	# Compute start and end values
	# Japanese is first so its start is 0 and end is just its %
	set_temp_variable = { spain_2_st = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { spain_2_end = 100 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { spain_2_end = IBR_choupana_influence }

	# Compute frame = SSSEEE by shifting over the start number 3 places
	# and adding the end number
	set_variable = { IBR_spain_2_frame = spain_2_st }
	round_variable = IBR_spain_2_frame
	multiply_variable = { IBR_spain_2_frame = 1000 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_spain_2_frame = spain_2_end }

	# Compute start and end values
	# choupana starts where spain_2 ends
	set_temp_variable = { choupana_st = spain_2_end }
	set_temp_variable = { choupana_end = choupana_st }
	add_to_temp_variable = { choupana_end = 100 }

	set_variable = { IBR_choupana_frame = choupana_st }
	round_variable = IBR_choupana_frame
	multiply_variable = { IBR_choupana_frame = 1000 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_choupana_frame = choupana_end }
}
IBR_caetano_influence_change = {
	add_to_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence = IBR_caetano_influence_temp }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence_temp > 0 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_caetano_influence_change_increase_tt
	}
	else = { custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_caetano_influence_change_decrease_tt }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = IBR_caetano_influence
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		set_temp_variable = { caetano_choupana_addition = IBR_caetano_influence }
		add_to_variable = { caetano_choupana_addition = IBR_choupana_influence }

		set_temp_variable = { new_stab_value = caetano_choupana_addition }
		multiply_temp_variable = { new_stab_value = 0.1 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { new_stab_value = -7.5 }
		divide_temp_variable = { new_stab_value = 30 }
		set_variable = { IBR_caetano_springboard_stab_var = new_stab_value }

		set_temp_variable = { new_pp_value = caetano_choupana_addition }
		multiply_temp_variable = { new_pp_value = 0.15 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { new_pp_value = -15 }
		divide_temp_variable = { new_pp_value = 60 }
		set_variable = { IBR_caetano_springboard_pp_var = new_pp_value }
	}
	IBR_caetano_springboard_calculation = yes
}

IBR_choupana_influence_change = {
	add_to_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence = IBR_choupana_influence_temp }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence_temp > 0 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_choupana_influence_change_increase_tt
	}
	else = { custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_choupana_influence_change_decrease_tt }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = IBR_choupana_influence
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence_temp = 500 }
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = paternalism_1
			popularity = IBR_choupana_influence_temp
		}

		set_temp_variable = { caetano_choupana_addition = IBR_caetano_influence }
		add_to_variable = { caetano_choupana_addition = IBR_choupana_influence }

		set_temp_variable = { new_stab_value = caetano_choupana_addition }
		multiply_temp_variable = { new_stab_value = 0.1 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { new_stab_value = -7.5 }
		divide_temp_variable = { new_stab_value = 30 }
		set_variable = { IBR_caetano_springboard_stab_var = new_stab_value }

		set_temp_variable = { new_pp_value = caetano_choupana_addition }
		multiply_temp_variable = { new_pp_value = 0.15 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { new_pp_value = -15 }
		divide_temp_variable = { new_pp_value = 60 }
		set_variable = { IBR_caetano_springboard_pp_var = new_pp_value }
	}
	IBR_caetano_springboard_calculation = yes
}

IBR_calculate_influences_advisory_council = {
	clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_franco_dominant_flag
	clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_salazar_dominant_flag
	clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_franco_plurality_flag
	clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_salazar_plurality_flag
	clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_caudillo_tie_flag
	clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_hardliner_dominant_flag
	clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_moderate_dominant_flag
	clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_independent_dominant_flag
	clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_crony_dominant_flag
	clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_political_tie_flag

	set_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_franco_influence = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_salazar_influence = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_neutral_influence = 0 }

	set_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_franco_members = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_salazar_members = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_neutral_members = 0 }

	set_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_hardliner_members = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_moderate_members = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_independent_members = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members = 0 }

	for_each_loop = {
		array = Iberian_Council_advisory_member_id
		value = v
		index = i
		break = break
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_political^i = 0 }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_hardliner_members = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_political^i = 1 }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_moderate_members = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_political^i = 2 }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_independent_members = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_political^i = 3 }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					NOT = {
						check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_political^i = 3 }
					}
					check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_caudillo^i = 0 }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_franco_members = 1 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_franco_influence = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					NOT = {
						check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_political^i = 3 }
					}
					check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_caudillo^i = 1 }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_salazar_members = 1 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_salazar_influence = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_political^i = 3 }
					check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_caudillo^i = 0 }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_franco_members = 1 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_franco_influence = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_political^i = 3 }
					check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_caudillo^i = 1 }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_salazar_members = 1 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_salazar_influence = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_member_caudillo^i = 2 }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_neutral_members = 1 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_neutral_influence = 1 }
		}
	}

	set_temp_variable = { franco_plus_neutrals_temp = Iberian_Council_advisory_franco_influence }
	add_to_temp_variable = { franco_plus_neutrals_temp = Iberian_Council_advisory_neutral_influence }
	set_temp_variable = { salazar_plus_neutrals_temp = Iberian_Council_advisory_salazar_influence }
	add_to_temp_variable = { salazar_plus_neutrals_temp = Iberian_Council_advisory_neutral_influence }

	set_temp_variable = { hardliners_plus_cronies_temp = Iberian_Council_advisory_hardliner_members }
	add_to_temp_variable = { hardliners_plus_cronies_temp = Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members }
	set_temp_variable = { moderates_plus_cronies_temp = Iberian_Council_advisory_moderate_members }
	add_to_temp_variable = { moderates_plus_cronies_temp = Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members }
	set_temp_variable = { independents_plus_cronies_temp = Iberian_Council_advisory_independent_members }
	add_to_temp_variable = { independents_plus_cronies_temp = Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members }

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_salazar_influence > 8 }
		}
		set_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_salazar_dominant_flag
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { salazar_plus_neutrals_temp > 8 }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_salazar_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_franco_members }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_salazar_plurality_flag
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_franco_members > 8 }
		}
		set_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_franco_dominant_flag
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { franco_plus_neutrals_temp > 8 }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_franco_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_salazar_members }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_franco_plurality_flag
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_caudillo_tie_flag
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { hardliners_plus_cronies_temp > 8 }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_hardliner_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_moderate_members }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_hardliner_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_independent_members }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_hardliner_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_hardliner_dominant_flag
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { moderates_plus_cronies_temp > 8 }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_moderate_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_hardliner_members }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_moderate_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_independent_members }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_moderate_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_moderate_dominant_flag
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { independents_plus_cronies_temp > 8 }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_independent_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_hardliner_members }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_independent_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_moderate_members }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_independent_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_independent_dominant_flag
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_hardliner_members }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_moderate_members }
				check_variable = { Iberian_Council_advisory_crony_members > Iberian_Council_advisory_independent_members }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_crony_dominant_flag
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_political_tie_flag
	}
}

IBR_change_council_influence = {
	add_to_variable = { Iberian_Council_influence_var = Iberian_Council_influence_var_temp }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = Iberian_Council_influence_var
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { Iberian_Council_influence_var_temp > 0 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = increase_Iberian_Council_influence_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { Iberian_Council_influence_var_temp < 0 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_influence_var_temp = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = decrease_Iberian_Council_influence_tt
	}
}

##New Iberian Council (Legislative)
IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular = {
	set_variable = { IBR_independent_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^0 }

	set_variable = { IBR_catholic_reformer_left = IBR_independent_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_catholic_reformer_right = IBR_catholic_reformer_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_catholic_reformer_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^1 }

	set_variable = { IBR_blue_reformer_left = IBR_catholic_reformer_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_blue_reformer_right = IBR_blue_reformer_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_blue_reformer_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^2 }

	set_variable = { IBR_modernist_left = IBR_blue_reformer_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_modernist_right = IBR_modernist_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_modernist_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^3 }

	set_variable = { IBR_moderate_conservative_left = IBR_modernist_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_moderate_conservative_right = IBR_moderate_conservative_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_moderate_conservative_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^4 }

	set_variable = { IBR_radical_conservative_left = IBR_moderate_conservative_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_radical_conservative_right = IBR_radical_conservative_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_radical_conservative_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^5 }

	set_variable = { IBR_technocrat_left = IBR_radical_conservative_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_technocrat_right = IBR_technocrat_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_technocrat_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^6 }

	set_variable = { IBR_monarchist_left = IBR_technocrat_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_monarchist_right = IBR_monarchist_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_monarchist_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^7 }

	set_variable = { IBR_traditionalist_left = IBR_monarchist_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_traditionalist_right = IBR_traditionalist_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_traditionalist_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^8 }

	set_variable = { IBR_fascist_left = IBR_traditionalist_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_fascist_right = IBR_fascist_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_fascist_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^9 }

	set_variable = { IBR_military_left = IBR_fascist_right }

	clear_array = iberianCouncil_seats
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 0
		size = IBR_independent_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 1
		size = IBR_catholic_reformer_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 2
		size = IBR_blue_reformer_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 3
		size = IBR_modernist_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 4
		size = IBR_moderate_conservative_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 5
		size = IBR_radical_conservative_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 6
		size = IBR_technocrat_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 7
		size = IBR_monarchist_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 8
		size = IBR_traditionalist_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 9
		size = IBR_fascist_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 10
		size = 450
	}
	IBR_set_up_legislative_council_initialize_positions = yes
}

IBR_rerender_legislative_council_support = {
	clear_array = iberianCouncil_seats

	#Part that supports bill (moves 20 pixels to the left)
	set_temp_variable = { total = 0 }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = 11
		value = i

		set_temp_variable = { ir = i }

		set_temp_variable = { old_total = total }
		add_to_temp_variable = { total = IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^ir }
		resize_array = {
			array = iberianCouncil_seats
			value = ir
			size = total
		}

		for_loop_effect = {
			start = old_total
			end = total
			value = k

			add_to_variable = { iberianCouncil_seats_x^k = -20 }
		}
	}
	#Part that votes blank on the bill (stays in place)
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = 11
		value = i

		set_temp_variable = { ir = i }

		set_temp_variable = { old_total = total }
		add_to_temp_variable = { total = IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^ir }
		resize_array = {
			array = iberianCouncil_seats
			value = ir
			size = total
		}

		for_loop_effect = {
			start = old_total
			end = total
			value = k

			add_to_variable = { iberianCouncil_seats_x^k = 0 }
		}
	}
	#Part that does not support bill (moves 20 pixels to the right)
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = 11
		value = i

		set_temp_variable = { ir = i }

		set_temp_variable = { old_total = total }

		set_temp_variable = { non_supporting_seats = IBR_council_faction_seats^ir }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { non_supporting_seats = IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^ir }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { non_supporting_seats = IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^ir }
		add_to_temp_variable = { total = non_supporting_seats }


		resize_array = {
			array = iberianCouncil_seats
			value = ir
			size = total
		}

		for_loop_effect = {
			start = old_total
			end = total
			value = k

			add_to_variable = { iberianCouncil_seats_x^k = 20 }

		}
	}
}

IBR_legislative_council_update_seat_order = {
	#0 Reformists
	#1 Conservatives
	#2 Hardliners
	find_highest_in_array = {
		array = IBR_council_total_faction_seats
		index = i
	}
	if = { # find_highest_in_array defaults to the first index in case of a tie, so 2 must be larger than 0 and 1, but 1 may be equal to 2, and 0 could be tied 3 ways
		limit = { check_variable = { i = 2 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 } }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_biggest_group = 2 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_second_biggest_group = 0 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_last_group = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { IBR_council_biggest_group = 2 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_second_biggest_group = 1 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_last_group = 0 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { i = 1 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 } }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_biggest_group = 2 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_second_biggest_group = 1 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_last_group = 0 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 } }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_biggest_group = 1 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_second_biggest_group = 0 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_last_group = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { IBR_council_biggest_group = 1 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_second_biggest_group = 2 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_last_group = 0 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
				check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
			}
			set_variable = { IBR_council_biggest_group = 2 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_second_biggest_group = 1 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_last_group = 0 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 } }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_biggest_group = 2 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_second_biggest_group = 0 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_last_group = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 } }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_biggest_group = 1 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_second_biggest_group = 0 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_last_group = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 } }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_biggest_group = 0 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_second_biggest_group = 1 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_last_group = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { IBR_council_biggest_group = 0 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_second_biggest_group = 2 }
			set_variable = { IBR_council_last_group = 1 }
		}
	}
}

IBR_legislative_council_distribute_subfaction_seats = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = IBR_council_independent_proportion
			}
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_council_independent_proportion = 0.1 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_council_independent_proportion = IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_independent_subfaction }
		divide_variable = { IBR_council_independent_proportion = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = IBR_council_catholic_reformer_proportion
			}
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_council_catholic_reformer_proportion = 0.25 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_council_catholic_reformer_proportion = IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_catholic_reformer_subfaction }
		divide_variable = { IBR_council_catholic_reformer_proportion = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = IBR_council_blue_reformer_proportion
			}
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_council_blue_reformer_proportion = 0.3 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_council_blue_reformer_proportion = IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_blue_reformer_subfaction }
		divide_variable = { IBR_council_blue_reformer_proportion = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_reformist_seats_assigned = 0 }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_independent_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_independent_seats = IBR_council_independent_proportion }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_independent_seats
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_independent_subfaction = IBR_council_independent_seats }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_reformist_seats_assigned = IBR_council_independent_seats }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_catholic_reformer_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_catholic_reformer_seats = IBR_council_catholic_reformer_proportion }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_catholic_reformer_seats
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_catholic_reformer_subfaction = IBR_council_catholic_reformer_seats }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_reformist_seats_assigned = IBR_council_catholic_reformer_seats }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_blue_reformer_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_blue_reformer_seats = IBR_council_blue_reformer_proportion }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_blue_reformer_seats
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_blue_reformer_subfaction = IBR_council_blue_reformer_seats }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_reformist_seats_assigned = IBR_council_blue_reformer_seats }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_modernist_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_modernist_seats = IBR_Council_reformist_seats_assigned }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_modernist_subfaction = IBR_council_modernist_seats }

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = IBR_council_moderate_conservative_proportion
			}
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_council_moderate_conservative_proportion = 0.3 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_council_moderate_conservative_proportion = IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_moderate_conservative_subfaction }
		divide_variable = { IBR_council_moderate_conservative_proportion = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = IBR_council_technocrat_proportion
			}
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_proportion = 0.25 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_proportion = IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_technocrat_subfaction }
		divide_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_proportion = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = IBR_council_monarchist_proportion
			}
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_proportion = 0.15 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_proportion = IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_monarchist_subfaction }
		divide_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_proportion = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_conservative_seats_assigned = 0 }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_moderate_conservative_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_moderate_conservative_seats = IBR_council_moderate_conservative_proportion }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_moderate_conservative_seats
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_moderate_conservative_subfaction = IBR_council_moderate_conservative_seats }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_conservative_seats_assigned = IBR_council_moderate_conservative_seats }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_seats = IBR_council_technocrat_proportion }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_technocrat_seats
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_technocrat_subfaction = IBR_council_technocrat_seats }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_conservative_seats_assigned = IBR_council_technocrat_seats }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_seats = IBR_council_monarchist_proportion }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_monarchist_seats
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_monarchist_subfaction = IBR_council_monarchist_seats }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_conservative_seats_assigned = IBR_council_monarchist_seats }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_radical_conservative_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_radical_conservative_seats = IBR_Council_conservative_seats_assigned }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_radical_conservative_subfaction = IBR_council_radical_conservative_seats }

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = IBR_council_traditionalist_proportion
			}
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_proportion = 0.35 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_proportion = IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_traditionalist_subfaction }
		divide_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_proportion = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = IBR_council_military_hardliner_proportion
			}
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_council_military_hardliner_proportion = 0.15 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_council_military_hardliner_proportion = IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_military_hardliner_subfaction }
		divide_variable = { IBR_council_military_hardliner_proportion = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_hardliner_seats_assigned = 0 }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_seats = IBR_council_traditionalist_proportion }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_traditionalist_seats
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_traditionalist_subfaction = IBR_council_traditionalist_seats }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_hardliner_seats_assigned = IBR_council_traditionalist_seats }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_military_hardliner_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_military_hardliner_seats = IBR_council_military_hardliner_proportion }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_military_hardliner_seats
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_military_hardliner_subfaction = IBR_council_military_hardliner_seats }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_Council_hardliner_seats_assigned = IBR_council_military_hardliner_seats }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_fascist_seats = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_fascist_seats = IBR_Council_hardliner_seats_assigned }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^IBR_council_fascist_subfaction = IBR_council_fascist_seats }

	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular = yes
}

IBR_legislative_council_election_1963 = {
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 85 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = 250 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 115 }

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_political_power < 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = -10 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_political_power > 50
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = -5 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = 10 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = -5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBRIberiaStability < 3 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 10 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = -25 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 15 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBRIberiaStability < 7 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 7 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = -10 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 3 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = -2 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = -3 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_stability < 0.30
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = -10 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_stability > 0.67
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = -5 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = 10 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = -5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = poverty_rate
				value = 30
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = -6 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = 10 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = -4 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = poverty_rate
				value = 30
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = poverty_rate
				value = 70
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 4 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = -5 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = poverty_rate
				value = 70
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 7 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = -15 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 8 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = base_inflation_rate
				value = 2
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = -5 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = 7 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = -2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = base_inflation_rate
				value = 2
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = base_inflation_rate
				value = 6
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = -7 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = base_inflation_rate
				value = 6
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 10 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = -15 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = GDP_growth_real
				value = 2
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 4 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = -5 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = GDP_growth_real
				value = 2
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = GDP_growth_real
				value = 4
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = -1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = GDP_growth_real
				value = 4
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = -3 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = 7 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = -4 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = PPI_Rebel_Victory
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 4 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = -2 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = -2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = PPI_Balaguer_Victory
				has_global_flag = PPI_ceasefire_successful
			}

		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = -2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = PPI_Trujillo_Victory
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = -4 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = 2 }
	}
	IBR_legislative_council_distribute_subfaction_seats = yes
}

IBR_legislative_council_change_seats = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = IBR_council_faction_seats^losing_group
				value = seat_change
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^losing_group = seat_change }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^winning_group = seat_change }
		set_temp_variable = { losing_faction_new_seats = IBR_council_faction_seats^losing_group }
		set_temp_variable = { winning_faction_new_seats = IBR_council_faction_seats^winning_group }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { losing_faction_new_seats = seat_change }
		add_to_temp_variable = { winning_faction_new_seats = seat_change }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { losing_group = 0 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 1 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 2 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 3 }
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = seat_change }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { losing_group = 4 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 5 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 6 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 7 }
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = seat_change }
		}
		else = {
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = seat_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { winning_group = 0 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 1 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 2 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 3 }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = seat_change }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { winning_group = 4 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 5 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 6 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 7 }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = seat_change }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = seat_change }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change = IBR_council_faction_seats^losing_group }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^losing_group = seat_change }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^winning_group = seat_change }
		set_temp_variable = { losing_faction_new_seats = IBR_council_faction_seats^losing_group }
		set_temp_variable = { winning_faction_new_seats = IBR_council_faction_seats^winning_group }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { losing_faction_new_seats = seat_change }
		add_to_temp_variable = { winning_faction_new_seats = seat_change }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { losing_group = 0 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 1 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 2 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 3 }
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = seat_change }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { losing_group = 4 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 5 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 6 }
					check_variable = { losing_group = 7 }
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = seat_change }
		}
		else = {
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = seat_change }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { winning_group = 0 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 1 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 2 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 3 }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = seat_change }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { winning_group = 4 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 5 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 6 }
					check_variable = { winning_group = 7 }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = seat_change }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 = seat_change }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { seat_change > 0 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_change_seats_tt
	}
	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular = yes
}

IBR_change_council_reformism = {
	set_temp_variable = { new_value_council_reformism = Iberian_Council_reformism_var }
	add_to_temp_variable = { new_value_council_reformism = Iberian_Council_reformism_var_temp }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = new_value_council_reformism
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	set_variable = { Iberian_Council_reformism_var = new_value_council_reformism }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { Iberian_Council_reformism_var_temp > 0 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = increase_Iberian_Council_reformism_tt
	}
	else = {
		multiply_temp_variable = { Iberian_Council_reformism_var_temp = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = decrease_Iberian_Council_reformism_tt
	}
}

		max = 100

	else = {
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = decrease_Iberian_Council_bill_reformism_tt

}

	if = {

			check_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp > 0 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = increase_Iberian_Council_bill_reformism_tt
	}
	else = {
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = decrease_Iberian_Council_bill_reformism_tt
	}
}

IBR_legislative_council_change_bill_seat_calculation_potential = {
	clear_array = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential
	clear_array = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential
	#iberianCouncil_seats^seat_idx
	set_temp_variable = { new_value_bill_reformism = IBR_council_active_law_reformism }
	add_to_temp_variable = { new_value_bill_reformism = IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = new_value_bill_reformism
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	add_to_temp_array = { seat_status = 0 }
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = seat_status
		value = 0
		size = 450
	}
	add_to_array = { supporting_seats_per_faction_potential = 0 }
	resize_array = {
		array = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential
		value = 0
		size = 11
	}
	add_to_array = { abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential = 0 }
	resize_array = {
		array = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential
		value = 0
		size = 11
	}
	set_temp_variable = { total_supporting_seats = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { inverted_law_reformism = 100 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { inverted_law_reformism = new_value_bill_reformism }
	divide_temp_variable = { inverted_law_reformism = 100 }

	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { total_supporting_seats < 225 }
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = iberianCouncil_seats
			value = current_seat_subfaction
			index = index_of_seat
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = seat_status^index_of_seat
						value = 1
						compare = not_equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { faction_reformism = current_seat_subfaction }
				divide_temp_variable = { faction_reformism = 10 }

				set_temp_variable = { general_probability = inverted_law_reformism }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { general_probability = faction_reformism }

				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { general_probability < 0 }
					}
					multiply_temp_variable = { general_probability = -1 }
				}

				set_temp_variable = { support_probability = 1 }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { support_probability = general_probability }
				multiply_temp_variable = { support_probability = 0.8 }

				set_temp_variable = { against_probability = general_probability }
				multiply_temp_variable = { against_probability = 0.8 }

				set_temp_variable = { abstention_probability = support_probability }
				add_to_temp_variable = { abstention_probability = against_probability }
				multiply_temp_variable = { abstention_probability = -1 }
				add_to_temp_variable = { abstention_probability = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { abstention_probability < 0 }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { abstention_probability = 0 }
				}
				random_list = {
					support_probability = {
						set_temp_variable = { seat_status^index_of_seat = 1 }
						add_to_variable = { supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^current_seat_subfaction = 1 }
						add_to_temp_variable = { total_supporting_seats = 1 }
					}
					abstention_probability = {
						set_temp_variable = { seat_status^index_of_seat = -1 }
						add_to_variable = { abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^current_seat_subfaction = 1 }
					}
					against_probability = {
						set_temp_variable = { seat_status^index_of_seat = -1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

IBR_legislative_council_apply_seat_changes_bill = {
	set_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = 0 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = 0 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = 450 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^0 = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^0 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^0 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^1 = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^1 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^1 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^2 = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^2 }

	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^8 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^9 = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^9 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^9 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^10 = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^10 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^10 }

	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^0 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^0 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^0 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^1 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^1 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^1 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^2 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^2 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^2 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^3 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^3 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^3 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^4 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^4 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^4 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^5 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^5 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^5 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^6 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^6 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^6 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^7 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^7 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^7 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^8 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^8 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^8 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^9 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^9 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^9 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstaining^10 = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^10 }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats = abstaining_seats_per_faction_potential^10 }

	subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_supporting_seats }
	subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_abstaining_seats }

	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular = yes
	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_support = yes
}

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_legislative_council_against_seats_potential_total = 450 }

	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_legislative_council_supporting_seats_potential_total = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^0 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_legislative_council_supporting_seats_potential_total = supporting_seats_per_faction_potential^1 }
