IBR_legislative_council_specific_group_show_tooltip_changing_position = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp < 11 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_specific_group_show_tooltip_changing_position_tt
		add_to_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = 1 }
		IBR_legislative_council_specific_group_show_tooltip_changing_position = yes
	}
}

IBR_legislative_council_calculate_initial_bill_position = {
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 50 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { Iberian_Council_reformism_var < 50 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = Iberian_Council_reformism_var }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 0.025 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = Iberian_Council_reformism_var }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 50 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 0.025 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp }
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = cau_strong_salazar
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 450 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 1 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 0.4 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 0.4 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = cau_weak_salazar
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 450 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 1 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 0.25 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp = 0.25 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = IBR_council_active_law_reformism_temp }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = cau_weak_franco
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism = 5 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = cau_strong_franco
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 10 }
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { # Reformist Plurality
				check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
				check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 15 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = { # Reformist-Conservative Tie
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
					}
					AND = { # Conservative Plurality
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
					}
					AND = { # Conservative-Hardliner Tie
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
					}
				}
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 2.5 }
		}
		else = { # Hardliner Plurality/Reformist-Hardliner Tie/Three Way Tie
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 7.5 }
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBRIberiaStability < 4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { # Reformist Plurality
				check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
				check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 5 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = { # Reformist-Conservative Tie
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
					}
					AND = { # Conservative Plurality
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
					}
					AND = { # Conservative-Hardliner Tie
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 1.5 }
		}
		else = { # Hardliner Plurality/Reformist-Hardliner Tie/Three Way Tie
			add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 3 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBRIberiaStability < 7 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { # Reformist Plurality
				check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
				check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = { # Reformist-Conservative Tie
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
					}
					AND = { # Conservative Plurality
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
					}
					AND = { # Conservative-Hardliner Tie
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
					}
				}
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 0.7 }
		}
		else = { # Hardliner Plurality/Reformist-Hardliner Tie/Three Way Tie
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 1.5 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { # Reformist Plurality
				check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
				check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 5 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = { # Reformist-Conservative Tie
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
					}
					AND = { # Conservative Plurality
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
					}
					AND = { # Conservative-Hardliner Tie
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 = IBR_council_total_faction_seats^2 }
						check_variable = { IBR_council_total_faction_seats^1 > IBR_council_total_faction_seats^0 }
					}
				}
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 1.5 }
		}
		else = { # Hardliner Plurality/Reformist-Hardliner Tie/Three Way Tie
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt = 3 }
		}
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	set_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_reformism = IBR_council_active_law_reformism_tt }
	custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_calculate_initial_bill_position_tt
}

IBR_legislative_council_initial_bill_seat_calculation = {
	#iberianCouncil_seats^seat_idx
	add_to_temp_array = { seat_status = 0 }
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = seat_status
		value = 0
		size = 450
	}
	add_to_temp_array = { supporting_seats_per_faction = 0 }
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = supporting_seats_per_faction
		value = 0
		size = 11
	}
	add_to_temp_array = { abstaining_seats_per_faction = 0 }
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = abstaining_seats_per_faction
		value = 0
		size = 11
	}
	set_temp_variable = { total_supporting_seats = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { inverted_law_reformism = 100 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { inverted_law_reformism = IBR_council_active_law_reformism }
	divide_temp_variable = { inverted_law_reformism = 100 }

	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { total_supporting_seats < 225 }
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = iberianCouncil_seats
			value = current_seat_subfaction
			index = index_of_seat
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = seat_status^index_of_seat
						value = 1
						compare = not_equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { faction_reformism = current_seat_subfaction }
				divide_temp_variable = { faction_reformism = 10 }

				set_temp_variable = { general_probability = inverted_law_reformism }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { general_probability = faction_reformism }

				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { general_probability < 0 }
					}
					multiply_temp_variable = { general_probability = -1 }
				}

				set_temp_variable = { support_probability = 1 }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { support_probability = general_probability }
				multiply_temp_variable = { support_probability = 0.8 }

				set_temp_variable = { against_probability = general_probability }
				multiply_temp_variable = { against_probability = 0.8 }

				set_temp_variable = { abstention_probability = support_probability }
				add_to_temp_variable = { abstention_probability = against_probability }
				multiply_temp_variable = { abstention_probability = -1 }
				add_to_temp_variable = { abstention_probability = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { abstention_probability < 0 }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { abstention_probability = 0 }
				}
				random_list = {
					support_probability = {
						set_temp_variable = { seat_status^index_of_seat = 1 }
						add_to_temp_variable = { supporting_seats_per_faction^current_seat_subfaction = 1 }
						add_to_temp_variable = { total_supporting_seats = 1 }
					}
					abstention_probability = {
						set_temp_variable = { seat_status^index_of_seat = -1 }
						add_to_temp_variable = { abstaining_seats_per_faction^current_seat_subfaction = 1 }
					}
					against_probability = {
						set_temp_variable = { seat_status^index_of_seat = -1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_independent_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^0 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_catholic_reformer_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^1 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_blue_reformer_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^2 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_modernist_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^3 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_moderate_conservative_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^4 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_radical_conservative_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^5 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_technocrat_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^6 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_monarchist_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^7 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_traditionalist_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^8 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_fascist_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^9 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_military_hardliner_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = supporting_seats_per_faction^10 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_independent_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = abstaining_seats_per_faction^0 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_abstention_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_catholic_reformer_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = abstaining_seats_per_faction^1 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_abstention_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_blue_reformer_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = abstaining_seats_per_faction^2 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_abstention_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_modernist_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = abstaining_seats_per_faction^3 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_abstention_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_moderate_conservative_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = abstaining_seats_per_faction^4 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_abstention_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_radical_conservative_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = abstaining_seats_per_faction^5 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_abstention_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_technocrat_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = abstaining_seats_per_faction^6 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_abstention_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_monarchist_subfaction }
	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = abstaining_seats_per_faction^7 }
	set_temp_variable = { is_change = 0 }
	IBR_legislative_council_abstention_for_specific_group = yes

	set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_traditionalist_subfaction }

IBR_legislative_council_change_support_faction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				support_change > 0
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { max_support_change = IBR_council_faction_seats^current_subfaction_id_temp }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { max_support_change = IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			variable = support_change
			max = max_support_change
		}
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_id_temp }
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = support_change }
		set_temp_variable = { is_change = 1 }
		IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_change_support_faction_positive_tt
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { min_support_change = IBR_council_faction_seats^current_subfaction_id_temp }
		multiply_temp_variable = { min_support_change = -1 }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			variable = support_change
			min = min_support_change
		}
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_id_temp }
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = support_change }
		set_temp_variable = { is_change = 1 }
		IBR_legislative_council_subtract_support_for_specific_group = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_change_support_faction_negative_tt
	}
}

IBR_legislative_council_change_abstention_faction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				abstention_change > 0
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { max_abstention_change = IBR_council_faction_seats^current_subfaction_id_temp }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { max_abstention_change = IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			variable = abstention_change
			max = max_abstention_change
		}
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_id_temp }
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = abstention_change }
		set_temp_variable = { is_change = 1 }
		IBR_legislative_council_abstention_for_specific_group = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_change_abstention_faction_positive_tt
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { min_abstention_change = IBR_council_faction_seats^current_subfaction_id_temp }
		multiply_temp_variable = { min_abstention_change = -1 }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			variable = abstention_change
			min = min_abstention_change
		}
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_id_temp }
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = abstention_change }
		set_temp_variable = { is_change = 1 }
		IBR_legislative_council_subtract_abstention_for_specific_group = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_change_abstention_faction_negative_tt
	}
}

IBR_legislative_council_specific_group_show_tooltip_starting_position = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_show_seats_for_current_law_flag
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp < 11 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_specific_group_show_tooltip_starting_position_tt
			add_to_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = 1 }
			IBR_legislative_council_specific_group_show_tooltip_starting_position = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_specific_group_show_tooltip_starting_position_not_selected_tt
	}
}

IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular_groups = {
	set_variable = { IBR_group1_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^0 }

	set_variable = { IBR_group2_left = IBR_group1_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_group2_right = IBR_group2_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_group2_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^1 }

	set_variable = { IBR_group3_left = IBR_catholic_reformer_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_group3_right = IBR_group3_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_group3_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^2 }

	set_variable = { IBR_group4_left = IBR_group3_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_group4_right = IBR_group4_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_group4_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^3 }

	set_variable = { IBR_group5_left = IBR_group4_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_group5_right = IBR_group5_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_group5_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^4 }

	set_variable = { IBR_group6_left = IBR_group5_right }
	set_variable = { IBR_group6_right = IBR_group6_left }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_group6_right = IBR_council_faction_seats^5 }

	set_variable = { IBR_group7_left = IBR_group6_right }

	clear_array = iberianCouncil_seats
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 0
		size = IBR_group1_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 1
		size = IBR_group2_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 2
		size = IBR_group3_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 3
		size = IBR_group4_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 4
		size = IBR_group5_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 5
		size = IBR_group6_right
	}
	resize_array = {
		array = iberianCouncil_seats
		value = 6
		size = 450
	}
	IBR_set_up_legislative_council_initialize_positions = yes
}

	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_split = 0.5 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_technocrat_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^2 = IBR_council_technocrat_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_6_assigned = IBR_council_technocrat_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^6 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_split = 0.15 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_technocrat_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^3 = IBR_council_technocrat_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_6_assigned = IBR_council_technocrat_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^6 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_split = 0.15 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_technocrat_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^4 = IBR_council_technocrat_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_6_assigned = IBR_council_technocrat_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^6 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_technocrat_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_6_assigned }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^6 = IBR_council_technocrat_split }

	#monarchist split
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^7 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_split = 0.15 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_monarchist_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^1 = IBR_council_monarchist_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_7_assigned = IBR_council_monarchist_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^7 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_split = 0.2 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_monarchist_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^2 = IBR_council_monarchist_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_7_assigned = IBR_council_monarchist_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^7 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_split = 0.30 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_monarchist_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^5 = IBR_council_monarchist_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_7_assigned = IBR_council_monarchist_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^7 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_monarchist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_7_assigned }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^6 = IBR_council_monarchist_split }

	#traditionalist split
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^8 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_split = 0.1 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_traditionalist_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^2 = IBR_council_traditionalist_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_8_assigned = IBR_council_traditionalist_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^8 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_split = 0.4 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_traditionalist_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^5 = IBR_council_traditionalist_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_8_assigned = IBR_council_traditionalist_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^8 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_traditionalist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_8_assigned }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^6 = IBR_council_traditionalist_split }

	#fascist split
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_fascist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^9 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_fascist_split = 0.05 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_fascist_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^2 = IBR_council_fascist_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_9_assigned = IBR_council_fascist_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_fascist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^9 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_fascist_split = 0.20 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_fascist_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^4 = IBR_council_fascist_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_9_assigned = IBR_council_fascist_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_fascist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^9 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_council_fascist_split = 0.1 }
	round_temp_variable = IBR_council_fascist_split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^5 = IBR_council_fascist_split }
	add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_9_assigned = IBR_council_fascist_split }

	set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_fascist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^9 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { IBR_council_fascist_split = IBR_council_faction_seats_9_assigned }
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^6 = IBR_council_fascist_split }

	#military hardliner split
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats^6 = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp^10 }
}

IBR_legislative_council_create_groups = {

	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular = yes

	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^1 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^2 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^3 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^4 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^5 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^6 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^7 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^8 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^9 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats^10 }

	clear_array = IBR_council_faction_seats

	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_seats = 0 }

	clear_array = IBR_council_faction_color

	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_color = 12 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_color = 13 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_color = 14 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_color = 15 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_color = 16 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_color = 17 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_faction_color = 18 }

	set_country_flag = IBR_council_parliamentary_groups_created
	clr_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_created_flag

	clear_array = IBR_council_current_group
	clear_array = IBR_council_column1_factions
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_column1_factions = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_column1_factions = 1 }
	resize_array = { array = IBR_council_current_group value = 4 size = 2 }
	clear_array = IBR_council_column2_factions
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_column2_factions = 2 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_column2_factions = 3 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_column2_factions = 4 }
	resize_array = { array = IBR_council_current_group value = 4 size = 5 }
	clear_array = IBR_council_column3_factions
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_column3_factions = 5 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_council_column3_factions = 6 }
	resize_array = { array = IBR_council_current_group value = 4 size = 7 }

	IBR_legislative_council_distribute_seats_groups = yes

	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular_groups = yes

	clear_array = IBR_council_faction_seats_temp

	remove_mission = IBR_reformism_tick_mission
}

IBR_council_introduce_bill = {
	set_country_flag = IBR_council_active_law_flag
	set_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_var = IBR_council_active_law_temp }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^0 = -2 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^1 = -2 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_accepted_investments = 0 }
	if = { # Advisory Council
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			activate_mission = IBR_advisory_council_deliberation_mission_cosmetic
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_council_law_when_passed_tt
	}
	else_if = { # Legislative Council
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_created_flag
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			activate_mission = IBR_legislative_council_deliberation_mission_cosmetic
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_council_law_when_submitted_legislative_tt
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_id_temp = IBR_council_independent_subfaction }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_show_seats_for_current_law_flag
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_specific_group_show_tooltip_starting_position_not_selected_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_legislative_council_current_seat_position_tt
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			IBR_legislative_council_calculate_initial_bill_position = yes
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_council_law_when_passed_legislative_tt
	}
	else_if = { # Democratic Council
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_democratic_council_created_flag
		}
		activate_mission = IBR_democratic_council_deliberation_mission_cosmetic
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_council_law_when_submitted_legislative_tt
		IBR_democratic_council_starting_values_lookup = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_council_law_if_passed_legislative_tt
	}
	effect_tooltip = {
		meta_effect = {
			text = { [ACTIVE_LAW]_effect = yes }
			ACTIVE_LAW = "[?IBR_council_active_law_temp.GetTokenKey]"
		}
	}
	# Special Cases
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_temp = token:IBR_law_rd_investment }
				check_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_temp = token:IBR_law_national_sponsorship }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_council_law_investments_5_tt
		set_country_flag = IBR_council_investment_bill
		set_variable = { IBR_council_max_investments = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_temp = token:IBR_law_national_development }
				check_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_temp = token:IBR_law_national_infrastructure }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_council_law_investments_7_tt
		set_country_flag = IBR_council_investment_bill
		set_variable = { IBR_council_max_investments = 5 }
	}
}

IBR_council_advance_bill_to_vote = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag
			}
			remove_mission = IBR_advisory_council_deliberation_mission_cosmetic
			activate_mission = IBR_advisory_council_deliberation_mission
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_created_flag
			}
			remove_mission = IBR_legislative_council_deliberation_mission_cosmetic
			activate_mission = IBR_legislative_council_deliberation_mission
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_democratic_council_created_flag
			}
			remove_mission = IBR_democratic_council_deliberation_mission_cosmetic
			activate_mission = IBR_democratic_council_deliberation_mission
		}
	}
}

IBR_council_add_amendment = {
	set_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^IBR_amendment_slot_temp = -1 }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_amendments^IBR_amendment_slot_temp = IBR_amendment_temp }
}

IBR_council_accept_amendment = {
	set_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^IBR_amendment_slot_temp = 1 }
	custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_council_amendment_when_accepted_tt
	effect_tooltip = {
		IBR_council_pass_amendment = yes
	}
}

IBR_council_reject_amendment = {
	set_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^IBR_amendment_slot_temp = 0 }
}

IBR_council_pass_amendment = {
	meta_effect = {
		text = { [AMENDMENT]_effect = yes }
		AMENDMENT = "[?IBR_council_active_law_amendments^IBR_amendment_slot_temp.GetTokenKey]"
	}
}

IBR_council_add_investment = {
	set_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_amendments^IBR_council_accepted_investments = IBR_investment_temp }
	set_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^IBR_council_accepted_investments = 1 }
	custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_council_investment_when_accepted_tt
	meta_effect = {
		text = { [INVESTMENT]_effect = yes }
		INVESTMENT = "[?IBR_investment_temp.GetTokenKey]"
	}
	add_to_variable = { IBR_council_accepted_investments = 1 }
}

IBR_council_pass_law = { #Indents screwed, not changing it now
	meta_effect = {
		text = { [ACTIVE_LAW]_effect = yes }
		ACTIVE_LAW = "[?IBR_council_active_law_var.GetTokenKey]"
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_paragraph_break_tt

	#First Amendment
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^0 = 1
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_amendment_slot_temp = 0 }
		IBR_council_pass_amendment = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_paragraph_break_tt
	}

	#Second Amendment
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^1 = 1
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_amendment_slot_temp = 1 }
		IBR_council_pass_amendment = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_paragraph_break_tt
	}

	#Investment Amendment Slots
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^2 = 1
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_amendment_slot_temp = 2 }
		IBR_council_pass_amendment = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_paragraph_break_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^3 = 1
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_amendment_slot_temp = 3 }
		IBR_council_pass_amendment = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_paragraph_break_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				IBR_council_active_law_amendments_state^4 = 1
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_amendment_slot_temp = 4 }
		IBR_council_pass_amendment = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_paragraph_break_tt
	}
}

IBR_legislative_council_support_for_specific_group = {
	#current_subfaction_id_temp
	#current_subfaction_seats_temp
	#is_change
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { is_change = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp > current_subfaction_seats_temp
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { new_seat_support_count = IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp }
			add_to_temp_variable = { new_seat_support_count = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstain_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp }
			set_temp_variable = { new_seat_support_count = IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp }
			add_to_temp_variable = { new_seat_support_count = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstain_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = 450 }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_supporting_seats }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_abstain_seats }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		set_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = 450 }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_supporting_seats }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_abstain_seats }
	}
	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular = yes
	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_support = yes
}

IBR_legislative_council_abstention_for_specific_group = {
	#current_subfaction_id_temp
	#current_subfaction_seats_temp
	#is_change
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { is_change = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp > current_subfaction_seats_temp
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { new_seat_abstain_count = IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp }
			add_to_temp_variable = { new_seat_abstain_count = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstain_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp }
			set_temp_variable = { new_seat_abstain_count = IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp }
			add_to_temp_variable = { new_seat_abstain_count = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstain_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
			subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		}
		set_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = 450 }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_supporting_seats }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_abstain_seats }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstain_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		set_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = 450 }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_supporting_seats }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_abstain_seats }
	}
	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular = yes
	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_support = yes
}

IBR_legislative_council_subtract_support_for_specific_group = {
	#current_subfaction_id_temp
	#current_subfaction_seats_temp
	set_temp_variable = { new_seat_support_count = IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp > IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { new_seat_support_count = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_supporting^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { new_seat_support_count = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
	}
	set_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = 450 }
	subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_supporting_seats }
	subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_abstain_seats }
	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular = yes
	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_support = yes
}

IBR_legislative_council_subtract_abstention_for_specific_group = {
	#current_subfaction_id_temp
	#current_subfaction_seats_temp
	set_temp_variable = { new_seat_abstain_count = IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp > IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { current_subfaction_seats_temp = IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { new_seat_abstain_count = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstain_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_faction_seats_abstain^current_subfaction_id_temp = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { new_seat_abstain_count = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_abstain_seats = current_subfaction_seats_temp }
	}
	set_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = 450 }
	subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_supporting_seats }
	subtract_from_variable = { IBR_council_total_against_seats = IBR_council_total_abstain_seats }
	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_regular = yes
	IBR_rerender_legislative_council_support = yes
}
### PRE-ELECTION MECHANICS ###
IBR_pre_election_setup = {
	activate_mission = IBR_pre_election_check_mission
	set_variable = { IBR_pre_election_opposition_influence = 25 }
	set_variable = { IBR_pre_election_government_influence = 50 }
	set_variable = { IBR_pre_election_conservatives_influence = 25 }

	set_variable = { IBR_pre_election_opposition_min_gain = 2 }
	set_variable = { IBR_pre_election_opposition_max_gain = 6 }

	set_variable = { IBR_pre_election_conservatives_min_gain = 3 }
	set_variable = { IBR_pre_election_conservatives_max_gain = 5 }

	set_variable = { IBR_transition_result = 0 }
	set_variable = { IBR_AP_ideology = 0 }
	set_variable = { IBR_CD_ideology = 0 }

	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score = 0 }

	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	add_to_array = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls = 0 }
	IBR_run_polling_update = yes
}
IBR_pre_election_change_influence = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Opposition
			check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_category_temp = 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp = 0 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp = IBR_pre_election_government_influence }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_influence }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp = 0 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp = IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp = IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_1_temp = IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_1_temp = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_influence }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_pre_election_conservatives_influence = IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_1_temp }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_2_temp = IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_2_temp = IBR_pre_election_government_influence }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_pre_election_government_influence = IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_2_temp }

		add_to_variable = { IBR_pre_election_opposition_influence = IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp > 0 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_influence_opposition_positive_tt
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp = -1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_influence_opposition_negative_tt
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { #Conservatives
			check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_category_temp = 2 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp = 0 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp = IBR_pre_election_government_influence }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp = IBR_pre_election_opposition_influence }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp = 0 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp = IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp = IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_1_temp = IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_1_temp = IBR_pre_election_opposition_influence }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_pre_election_opposition_influence = IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_1_temp }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_2_temp = IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_2_temp = IBR_pre_election_government_influence }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_pre_election_government_influence = IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_2_temp }

		add_to_variable = { IBR_pre_election_conservatives_influence = IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp > 0 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_influence_conservative_positive_tt
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp = -1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_influence_conservative_negative_tt
		}
	}
	else = { #Government
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp = 0 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_influence }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp = IBR_pre_election_opposition_influence }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp = 0 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp = IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp }
		divide_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp = IBR_pre_election_others_sum_temp }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_1_temp = IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_1_temp = IBR_pre_election_opposition_influence }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_pre_election_opposition_influence = IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_1_temp }

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_2_temp = IBR_pre_election_ratio_temp }
		multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_2_temp = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_influence }
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_pre_election_conservatives_influence = IBR_pre_election_others_ratio_2_temp }

		add_to_variable = { IBR_pre_election_government_influence = IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp > 0 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_influence_government_positive_tt
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp = -1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_influence_government_negative_tt
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = IBR_pre_election_government_influence
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_influence
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = IBR_pre_election_opposition_influence
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	#50% check
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_opposition_influence > 49.99 }
		}
		country_event = { id = IBR_pre_election.44 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_conservatives_influence > 49.99 }
		}
		country_event = { id = IBR_pre_election.45 }
	}
}

IBR_pre_election_change_min = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Opposition
			check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_category_temp = 1 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_pre_election_opposition_min_gain = IBR_pre_election_change_min_temp_var }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = IBR_pre_election_opposition_min_gain
			min = 0
			max = IBR_pre_election_opposition_max_gain
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_change_min_temp_var > 0 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_min_opposition_positive_tt
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_change_min_temp_var = -1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_min_opposition_negative_tt
		}
	}
	else = { #Conservatives
		add_to_variable = { IBR_pre_election_conservatives_min_gain = IBR_pre_election_change_min_temp_var }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_min_gain
			min = 0
			max = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_max_gain
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_change_min_temp_var > 0 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_min_conservative_positive_tt
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_change_min_temp_var = -1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_min_conservative_negative_tt
		}
	}
}

IBR_pre_election_change_max = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Opposition
			check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_category_temp = 1 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { IBR_pre_election_opposition_max_gain = IBR_pre_election_change_max_temp_var }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = IBR_pre_election_opposition_max_gain
			min = IBR_pre_election_opposition_min_gain
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_change_max_temp_var > 0 }
			}

		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_change_max_temp_var = -1 }

		}
	}
	else = { #Conservatives
		add_to_variable = { IBR_pre_election_conservatives_max_gain = IBR_pre_election_change_max_temp_var }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_max_gain
			min = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_min_gain
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_pre_election_change_max_temp_var > 0 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_max_conservative_positive_tt
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_change_max_temp_var = -1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_change_max_conservative_negative_tt
		}
	}
}

IBR_modify_party_popularity_pre_election = {
	add_to_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^faction_number = popularity_change }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				popularity_change > 0
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_modify_party_popularity_pre_election_positive_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_modify_party_popularity_pre_election_negative_tt
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^faction_number
		min = 0
	}
	IBR_run_polling_update = yes
}

IBR_run_polling_update = {
	clear_temp_array = IBR_poll_points_temp
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_total_poll_points = 0 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^0 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^1 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^2 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^3 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^4 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^5 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^6 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^7 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^8 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^9 }
	add_to_temp_array = { IBR_poll_points_temp = IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^10 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^0 < 2 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^0 = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^1 < 2 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^1 = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^2 < 4 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^2 = 4 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^3 < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^3 = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^4 < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^4 = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^5 < 6 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^5 = 6 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^6 < 4 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^6 = 4 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^7 < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^7 = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^8 < 6 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^8 = 6 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^9 < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^9 = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_score^10 < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_poll_points_temp^10 = 1 }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = IBR_poll_points_temp
		add_to_temp_variable = { IBR_total_poll_points = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = IBR_poll_points_temp
		set_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls^i = v }
		divide_variable = { IBR_democratic_council_faction_polls^i = IBR_total_poll_points }
	}
}


### Indiviualized Effects for Every Bill + Amendment
# Migration Management Law
IBR_law_migration_management_effect = {
	add_stability = 0.025
	TNO_improve_immigration_policy_effectiveness_low = yes
}
IBR_law_migration_management_am_c_1_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { temp_econ_spending_amount = 0.05 }
	econ_spend_money_once_effect_raw_money = yes
	IBR_improve_bureaucrats_opinion = yes
}
IBR_law_migration_management_am_h_1_effect = {
	IBR_improve_military_opinion = yes
	set_temp_variable = { temp_econ_spending_amount = 0.05 }
	econ_spend_money_once_effect_modal = yes
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			any_neighbor_state = {
				is_controlled_by = FRA
			}
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = bunker
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
			province = {
				all_provinces = yes
				limit_to_border_country = FRA
			}
		}
	}
}
IBR_law_migration_management_am_m_1_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { temp_econ_spending_amount = 0.15 }
	econ_spend_money_once_effect_raw_money = yes
	TNO_improve_immigration_policy_effectiveness_low = yes
	add_political_power = 15
}
IBR_law_migration_management_am_i_1_effect = {
	TNO_improve_admin_efficiency_really_low = yes
	TNO_improve_immigration_policy_effectiveness_high = yes
	set_temp_variable = { temp_econ_spending_amount = 0.035 }
	econ_spend_money_once_effect_modal = yes
}
IBR_law_migration_management_am_h_2_effect = {
	tno_immigration_worsen = yes
	add_political_power = -25
	add_stability = 0.025
	IBR_worsen_bureaucrats_opinion = yes
	TNO_worsen_admin_efficiency_low = yes
}
IBR_law_migration_management_am_m_2_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { temp_econ_spending_amount = 0.25 }
	econ_spend_money_once_effect_raw_money = yes
	add_political_power = -25

# Municipal Services Law
IBR_law_municipal_services_effect = {
	random_owned_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			is_core_of = IBR
			infrastructure < 10
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = infrastructure
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
		}
		add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
		set_temp_variable = { state_value_multiplier_temp = 1.01 }
		econ_state_value_change_multiply = yes
	}
	random_owned_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			is_core_of = IBR
			infrastructure < 10
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = infrastructure
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
		}

# Iberian Constitution (Authoritarian)
IBR_law_constitution_auth_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_iberian_labour_charter }
	TNO_remove_assorted_law = yes
	set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_organic_bases_of_the_creation_of_the_union }
	TNO_remove_assorted_law = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_iberian_council_law_adv }
		TNO_remove_assorted_law = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_iberian_council_law_leg }
		TNO_remove_assorted_law = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_iberian_charter }
	TNO_remove_assorted_law = yes
	set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_organic_law_of_the_state }
	TNO_remove_assorted_law = yes
	set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_fundamental_statute_of_the_iberian_union }
	TNO_add_assorted_law = yes
	add_political_power = 50
	add_stability = 0.1
	TNO_improve_admin_efficiency_med = yes
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_stability_temp = 9 }
	IBR_stability_change = yes
	#TNO_Country_Closing_Screen_Show = yes
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence_temp = 15 }
	IBR_caetano_influence_change = yes
	#set_cosmetic_tag = IBR_CONGRESS
}

# Iberian Constitution (Democratic)
IBR_law_constitution_dem_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_law_of_political_reform }
	TNO_remove_assorted_law = yes
	set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_organic_bases_of_the_creation_of_the_union }
	TNO_remove_assorted_law = yes
	set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_iberian_constitution }
	TNO_add_assorted_law = yes
	add_political_power = 100
	set_temp_variable = { IBR_stability_temp = 10 }
	IBR_stability_change = yes
	#TNO_Country_Closing_Screen_Show = yes
	set_cosmetic_tag = IBR_CONGRESS
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { # CD Results
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = IBRCentrist70Victory
			}
			if = { # Very Weak Ending
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_transition_result < -2 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_centrist.38
					days = 3
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Weak Ending
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_transition_result < 0 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_centrist.37
					days = 3
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Strong Ending
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_transition_result < 2.1 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_centrist.36
					days = 3
				}
			}
			else = { # Very Strong Ending
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_centrist.35
					days = 3
				}
			}
		}
		else = { # AP Results
			if = { # Very Weak Ending
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_transition_result < -2 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_natcon.38
					days = 3
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Weak Ending
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_transition_result < 0 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_natcon.37
					days = 3
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Strong Ending
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_transition_result < 2.1 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_natcon.36
					days = 3
				}
			}
			else = { # Very Strong Ending
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_natcon.35
					days = 3
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
